01

Hello CUDA

PG 3-5.1

PG 3-5.1
▶
week1_basics/day01_hello_cuda.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 01: Hello CUDA — 向量加法 * ================================ * 学习目标: * 1. 理解 CUDA 编程模型：Host (CPU) 与 Device (GPU) 的异构协作 * 2. 掌握 kernel 函数的编写 (__global__) * 3. 理解 grid / block / thread 的层次结构 * 4. 学会 cudaMalloc / cudaMemcpy / cudaFree 的基本内存管理 * * 核心概念: * - __global__: 在 device 上执行，从 host 调用 (可从 compute capability 3.x 起也从 device 调用) * - <<<gridSize, blockSize>>>: 执行配置，指定 grid 中 block 数量和每个 block 中 thread 数量 * - threadIdx.x / blockIdx.x / blockDim.x / gridDim.x: 内建变量，用于计算全局线程索引 * * 编译: nvcc day01_hello_cuda.cu -o day01 * 运行: ./day01 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
/* * Day 01: Hello CUDA — 向量加法 * ================================ * 学习目标: * 1. 理解 CUDA 编程模型：Host (CPU) 与 Device (GPU) 的异构协作 * 2. 掌握 kernel 函数的编写 (__global__) * 3. 理解 grid / block / thread 的层次结构 * 4. 学会 cudaMalloc / cudaMemcpy / cudaFree 的基本内存管理 * * 核心概念: * - __global__: 在 device 上执行，从 host 调用 (可从 compute capability 3.x 起也从 device 调用) * - <<<gridSize, blockSize>>>: 执行配置，指定 grid 中 block 数量和每个 block 中 thread 数量 * - threadIdx.x / blockIdx.x / blockDim.x / gridDim.x: 内建变量，用于计算全局线程索引 * * 编译: nvcc day01_hello_cuda.cu -o day01 * 运行: ./day01 */ #include <stdio.h> // ============================================================ // Kernel: 向量加法 C = A + B // 每个 thread 负责一个元素的加法 // ============================================================ __global__ void vectorAdd(const float *A, const float *B, float *C, int n) { // 计算当前 thread 的全局索引 int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 边界检查：防止越界访问 if (idx < n) { C[idx] = A[idx] + B[idx]; } }
Kernel 2
* Day 01: Hello CUDA — 向量加法 * ================================ * 学习目标: * 1. 理解 CUDA 编程模型：Host (CPU) 与 Device (GPU) 的异构协作 * 2. 掌握 kernel 函数的编写 (__global__) * 3. 理解 grid / block / thread 的层次结构 * 4. 学会 cudaMalloc / cudaMemcpy / cudaFree 的基本内存管理 * * 核心概念: * - __global__: 在 device 上执行，从 host 调用 (可从 compute capability 3.x 起也从 device 调用) * - <<<gridSize, blockSize>>>: 执行配置，指定 grid 中 block 数量和每个 block 中 thread 数量 * - threadIdx.x / blockIdx.x / blockDim.x / gridDim.x: 内建变量，用于计算全局线程索引 * * 编译: nvcc day01_hello_cuda.cu -o day01 * 运行: ./day01 */ #include <stdio.h> // ============================================================ // Kernel: 向量加法 C = A + B // 每个 thread 负责一个元素的加法 // ============================================================ __global__ void vectorAdd(const float *A, const float *B, float *C, int n) { // 计算当前 thread 的全局索引 int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 边界检查：防止越界访问 if (idx < n) { C[idx] = A[idx] + B[idx]; } }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // Kernel: 向量加法 C = A + B // 每个 thread 负责一个元素的加法 // ============================================================ __global__ void vectorAdd(const float *A, const float *B, float *C, int n) { // 计算当前 thread 的全局索引 int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 边界检查：防止越界访问 if (idx < n) { C[idx] = A[idx] + B[idx]; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { // --- 1. 设置向量大小 --- int n = 1 << 20; // 1M 个元素 size_t size = n * sizeof(float); printf("向量大小: %d 元素 (%.2f MB)\n\n", n, (float)size / 1e6); // --- 2. 分配 Host 内存并初始化 --- float *h_A = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_B = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_C = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_A[i] = (float)i * 0.001f; h_B[i] = (float)(n - i) * 0.001f; } // --- 3. 分配 Device 内存 --- float *d_A, *d_B, *d_C; cudaMalloc((void **)&d_A, size); cudaMalloc((void **)&d_B, size); cudaMalloc((void **)&d_C, size); // --- 4. 将数据从 Host 拷贝到 Device (H2D) --- cudaMemcpy(d_A, h_A, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); cudaMemcpy(d_B, h_B, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); // --- 5. 配置 kernel 执行参数 --- int blockSize = 256; // 每个 block 256 个 thread // 向上取整计算 grid 大小 int gridSize = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize; printf("Kernel 配置:\n"); printf(" Grid size: %d blocks\n", gridSize); printf(" Block size: %d threads\n", blockSize); printf(" 总线程数: %d\n\n", gridSize * blockSize); // --- 6. 启动 Kernel --- vectorAdd<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, n); // --- 7. 将结果从 Device 拷贝回 Host (D2H) --- cudaMemcpy(h_C, d_C, size, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); // --- 8. 验证结果 --- int errors = 0; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { float expected = h_A[i] + h_B[i]; if (fabs(h_C[i] - expected) > 1e-5f) { if (errors < 5) { printf(" 错误: h_C[%d] = %f, 期望 = %f\n", i, h_C[i], expected); } errors++; } } if (errors == 0) { printf("验证通过! 所有 %d 个元素计算正确。\n", n); } else { printf("验证失败! 共 %d 个错误。\n", errors); } // 打印几个示例值 printf("\n示例结果:\n"); printf(" h_C[0] = %f\n", h_C[0]); printf(" h_C[%d] = %f\n", n / 2, h_C[n / 2]); printf(" h_C[%d] = %f\n", n - 1, h_C[n - 1]); // --- 9. 释放内存 --- cudaFree(d_A); cudaFree(d_B); cudaFree(d_C); free(h_A); free(h_B); free(h_C); printf("\n✓ Day 01 完成! 你已经写了第一个 CUDA 程序。\n"); return 0; }
编译 & 运行
# 编译 nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 week1_basics/day01_hello_cuda.cu -o week1_basics/day01_hello_cuda # 运行 ./week1_basics/day01_hello_cuda

week1_basics/day02_device_query.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 02: GPU 设备查询 — 理解你的硬件 * ===================================== * 学习目标: * 1. 使用 CUDA Runtime API 查询 GPU 设备信息 * 2. 理解关键硬件参数对编程的影响 * 3. 学会根据硬件特性选择合适的 grid/block 配置 * * 关键参数: * - compute capability: 决定支持的特性集 * - multiProcessorCount (SM数): 决定并行度上限 * - maxThreadsPerBlock: 每 block 最大线程数 (通常 1024) * - sharedMemPerBlock: 每 block 共享内存大小 * - warpSize: warp 大小 (通常 32) * - maxThreadsDim[3]: 每 block 各维度最大线程数 * - maxGridSize[3]: grid 各维度最大 block 数 * * 编译: nvcc day02_device_query.cu -o day02 * 运行: ./day02 */
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { int deviceCount = 0; cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount); printf("============================================\n"); printf(" CUDA 设备查询 — 共 %d 个设备\n", deviceCount); printf("============================================\n\n"); for (int dev = 0; dev < deviceCount; dev++) { cudaDeviceProp prop; cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, dev); printf("--- 设备 %d ---\n", dev); printf(" 设备名称: %s\n", prop.name); printf(" 计算能力: %d.%d\n", prop.major, prop.minor); printf(" 总全局内存: %.2f GB\n", prop.totalGlobalMem / 1e9); printf(" SM 数量: %d\n", prop.multiProcessorCount); printf(" 每 SM 最大线程数: %d\n", prop.maxThreadsPerMultiProcessor); printf(" 每 block 最大线程数: %d\n", prop.maxThreadsPerBlock); printf(" Warp 大小: %d\n", prop.warpSize); printf(" 共享内存/block: %.2f KB\n", prop.sharedMemPerBlock / 1024.0); printf(" 共享内存/SM: %.2f KB\n", prop.sharedMemPerMultiprocessor / 1024.0); printf(" 常量内存: %.2f KB\n", prop.totalConstMem / 1024.0); printf(" L2 缓存: %.2f KB\n", prop.l2CacheSize / 1024.0); printf(" 内存时钟频率: %.2f GHz\n", prop.memoryClockRate / 1e6); printf(" 内存总线宽度: %d bits\n", prop.memoryBusWidth); printf(" 内存带宽: %.2f GB/s\n", 2.0 * prop.memoryClockRate * (prop.memoryBusWidth / 8) / 1e6); printf(" 最大 grid 维度: (%d, %d, %d)\n", prop.maxGridSize[0], prop.maxGridSize[1], prop.maxGridSize[2]); printf(" 最大 block 维度: (%d, %d, %d)\n", prop.maxThreadsDim[0], prop.maxThreadsDim[1], prop.maxThreadsDim[2]); printf(" 是否支持统一内存: %s\n", prop.unifiedAddressing ? "是" : "否"); printf(" 是否支持协作组: %s\n", prop.cooperativeLaunch ? "是" : "否"); printf(" 多 GPU 板: %s\n", prop.isMultiGpuBoard ? "是" : "否"); printf(" 异步引擎数量: %d\n", prop.asyncEngineCount); printf("\n"); // 计算理论最大并行线程数 int maxThreads = prop.multiProcessorCount * prop.maxThreadsPerMultiProcessor; printf(" ★ 理论最大并行线程: %d (%d 万)\n", maxThreads, maxThreads / 10000); printf(" ★ 建议 blockSize: 256 (warpSize 的整数倍)\n"); printf(" ★ 建议 gridSize: 取决于数据量，保证足够 block 充满所有 SM\n\n"); } // 演示如何根据硬件选择配置 if (deviceCount > 0) { cudaDeviceProp prop; cudaGetDeviceProperti // ... 向量运算

PG 5.2

PG 5.2
▶
week1_basics/day03_vector_ops.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 03: 向量运算 — 理解 Grid/Block 配置 * ========================================= * 学习目标: * 1. 理解不同 grid/block 配置对性能的影响 * 2. 实现多种向量运算 (缩放、fma、sigmoid) * 3. 学习使用 2D grid/block 处理 2D 数据 * 4. 理解 occupancy (占用率) 的概念 * * 关键概念: * - block size 应为 warpSize(32) 的整数倍 * - 太小的 block 会导致低 occupancy * - 太大的 block 会限制每 SM 可驻留的 block 数 * - 经验值: 128 或 256 通常是好选择 * * 编译: nvcc day03_vector_ops.cu -o day03 * 运行: ./day03 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 向量缩放 Y = alpha * X // ============================================================ __global__ void vectorScale(const float *X, float *Y, float alpha, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { Y[idx] = alpha * X[idx]; } }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Kernel 2: 融合乘加 Z = alpha * X + beta * Y (FMA) // 利用 GPU 的 FMA 指令，一条指令完成乘加 // ============================================================ __global__ void fusedMultiplyAdd(const float *X, const float *Y, float *Z, float alpha, float beta, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { Z[idx] = alpha * X[idx] + beta * Y[idx]; // 编译器会生成 FMA 指令 } }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // Kernel 3: Sigmoid 激活函数 (逐元素超越函数运算) // sigmoid(x) = 1 / (1 + exp(-x)) // 适合 GPU: 大量独立的计算 // ============================================================ __global__ void sigmoidKernel(const float *X, float *Y, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { Y[idx] = 1.0f / (1.0f + expf(-X[idx])); } }
Kernel 4
// ============================================================ // Kernel 4: 使用 2D grid 处理 1D 数据 (每个线程处理多个元素) // 这种 "grid-stride loop" 模式适用于数据量远大于线程数的情况 // ============================================================ __global__ void gridStrideLoop(const float *X, float *Y, float alpha, int n) { // 计算全局线程索引 int tid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 总线程数 int stride = gridDim.x * blockDim.x; // 每个线程以 stride 为步长遍历数据 for (int i = tid; i < n; i += stride) { Y[i] = alpha * X[i] * X[i]; // Y = alpha * X^2 } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { int n = 1 << 22; // 4M 元素 size_t size = n * sizeof(float); printf("数据量: %d 元素 (%.2f MB)\n\n", n, (float)size / 1e6); // 分配 Host 内存 float *h_X = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_Y1 = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_Y2 = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_Y3 = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_Y4 = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_X[i] = (float)(i % 1000) * 0.01f - 5.0f; // 范围 [-5, 5) } // 分配 Device 内存 float *d_X, *d_Y; cudaMalloc(&d_X, size); cudaMalloc(&d_Y, size); cudaMemcpy(d_X, h_X, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); // --- 测试不同 block size 的向量缩放 --- float alpha = 2.5f; int blockSizes[] = {32, 64, 128, 256, 512, 1024}; int numConfigs = sizeof(blockSizes) / sizeof(blockSizes[0]); printf("=== 向量缩放 Y = %.1f * X ===\n", alpha); for (int c = 0; c < numConfigs; c++) { int bs = blockSizes[c]; int gs = (n + bs - 1) / bs; // 使用 CUDA 事件计时 cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // 预热 vectorScale<<<gs, bs>>>(d_X, d_Y, alpha, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); // 运行 100 次取平均 for (int iter = 0; iter < 100; iter++) { vectorScale<<<gs, bs>>>(d_X, d_Y, alpha, n); } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms = 0; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); float avgMs = ms / 100.0f; float bandwidth = (2.0f * size) / (avgMs * 1e-3) / 1e9; // 读+写 printf(" blockSize=%4d grid=%6d 耗时=%7.3f ms 带宽=%6.1f GB/s\n", bs, gs, avgMs, bandwidth); cudaEventDestroy(start); cudaEventDestroy(stop); } // --- 测试 FMA --- printf("\n=== FMA: Z = 2.0*X + 0.5*X ===\n"); { int bs = 256; int gs = (n + bs - 1) / bs; fusedMultiplyAdd<<<gs, bs>>>(d_X, d_X, d_Y, 2.0f, 0.5f, n); cudaMemcpy(h_Y2, d_Y, size, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); // CPU 验证 bool ok = true; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { float expected = 2.0f * h_X[i] + 0.5f * h_X[i]; if (fabs(h_Y2[i] - expected) > 1e-4f) { ok = false; break; } } printf(" %s\n", ok ? "✓ 验证通过" : "✗ 验证失败"); } // --- 测试 Sigmoid --- printf("\n=== Sigmoid ===\n"); { int bs = 256; int gs = (n + bs - 1) / bs; sigmoidKernel<<<gs // ... (后续代码省略)

错误处理

PG 6.2.12, BPG 14

PG 6.2.12, BPG 14

week1_basics/day04_error_handling.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 04: CUDA 错误处理 — 写健壮的 GPU 代码 * =========================================== * 学习目标: * 1. 掌握 CUDA 错误检查的两种方式: 返回码和 kernel 执行错误 * 2. 编写可复用的错误检查宏 * 3. 理解常见的 CUDA 错误类型及其原因 * 4. 学会使用 cudaDeviceSynchronize() 捕获异步错误 * * 关键概念: * - CUDA Runtime API 函数返回 cudaError_t 错误码 * - Kernel 启动是异步的, 错误不会立即返回 * - 需要 cudaDeviceSynchronize() 或 cudaMemcpy 来捕获 kernel 错误 * - 开发阶段应检查每一个 CUDA 调用 * * 编译: nvcc day04_error_handling.cu -o day04 * 运行: ./day04 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// 简单 kernel __global__ void safeAdd(const float *A, const float *B, float *C, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { C[idx] = A[idx] + B[idx]; } }
Kernel 2
// 故意越界访问的 kernel (用于演示错误捕获) __global__ void badKernel(float *C, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 故意越界: 不做边界检查 C[idx] = 1.0f / C[idx + n]; // 越界! }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 04: CUDA 错误处理\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); // --- 1. 正常流程: 每一步都检查 --- printf("--- 1. 正常流程 (每步检查) ---\n"); int n = 1 << 20; size_t size = n * sizeof(float); float *h_A = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_B = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_C = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_A[i] = 1.0f; h_B[i] = 2.0f; } float *d_A, *d_B, *d_C; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_A, size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_B, size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_C, size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_A, h_A, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_B, h_B, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); safeAdd<<<(n + 255) / 256, 256>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, n); CUDA_CHECK_KERNEL(); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(h_C, d_C, size, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost)); printf(" ✓ 正常流程完成, h_C[0] = %f (期望 3.0)\n\n", h_C[0]); // --- 2. 模拟常见错误 --- printf("--- 2. 常见错误演示 ---\n\n"); // 错误 A: 分配过大内存 printf(" [A] 尝试分配 100GB 内存...\n"); float *d_huge; CUDA_CHECK_SOFT(cudaMalloc(&d_huge, (size_t)100 * 1024 * 1024 * 1024)); // 错误 B: 无效设备序号 printf("\n [B] 尝试设置不存在的设备...\n"); CUDA_CHECK_SOFT(cudaSetDevice(999)); // 错误 C: 配置过大的 block printf("\n [C] 尝试启动 blockSize=2048 (超过限制)...\n"); badKernel<<<1, 2048>>>(d_C, n); CUDA_CHECK_SOFT(cudaGetLastError()); // 错误 D: 越界访问 (需要同步才能捕获) printf("\n [D] 越界访问 kernel...\n"); badKernel<<<1, 256>>>(d_C, n); CUDA_CHECK_SOFT(cudaDeviceSynchronize()); // --- 3. 常见错误类型速查表 --- printf("\n--- 3. 常见 CUDA 错误速查 ---\n"); printf(" ┌─────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────┐\n"); printf(" │ 错误码 │ 常见原因 │\n"); printf(" ├─────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────┤\n"); printf(" │ cudaErrorMemoryAllocation │ 显存不足或请求过大 │\n"); printf(" │ cudaErrorInvalidValue │ 参数非法 (如负数大小) │\n"); printf(" │ cudaErrorInvalidDevice │ 设备序号不存在 │\n"); printf(" │ cudaErrorInvalidConfiguration│ grid/block 配置超出限制 │\n"); printf(" │ cudaErrorLaunchFailure │ kernel 执行失败 (如越界) │\n"); printf(" │ cudaErrorLaunchOutOfResources│ 寄存器/共享内存不足 │\n"); printf(" │ cudaErrorLaunchTimeout │ 执行超时 (可能被OS抢占) │\n"); p // ... GPU 计时

BPG 9.1

BPG 9.1
▶
week1_basics/day05_gpu_timer.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 05: GPU 计时 — 精确测量性能 * ================================= * 学习目标: * 1. 使用 CUDA Event 精确计时 GPU 操作 * 2. 理解 CPU 计时和 GPU 计时的区别 * 3. 学习预热(warmup)的重要性 * 4. 对比 CPU 和 GPU 的性能差异 * * 关键概念: * - CUDA Event 在 GPU 时间线上记录, 不受 CPU 时钟影响 * - cudaEventRecord() 是异步的, 需要 synchronize * - 首次 kernel 启动有初始化开销, 需要预热 * - 数据传输 H2D/D2H 的时间也需要计入 * * 编译: nvcc day05_gpu_timer.cu -o day05 * 运行: ./day05 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// 计算密集型 kernel: 每个元素做多次三角函数运算 __global__ void heavyCompute(const float *in, float *out, int n, int iters) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { float val = in[idx]; for (int i = 0; i < iters; i++) { val = sinf(val) + cosf(val * 0.5f); val = val * val; } out[idx] = val; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 05: GPU 计时与性能对比\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 22; // 4M 元素 int iters = 100; // 每元素 100 次三角函数运算 size_t size = n * sizeof(float); float *h_in = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_out = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_in[i] = (float)i * 0.0001f; } // --- CPU 计时 --- printf("--- CPU 计算 ---\n"); cudaEventCreate(&stopTotal); int blockSize = 256; int gridSize = (n + blockSize - 1) / blockSize; // 记录总时间 (包括数据传输) cudaEventRecord(startTotal); // H2D 传输 cudaMemcpy(d_in, h_in, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); // 预热 (首次 kernel 有开销) printf(" 预热中...\n"); heavyCompute<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_in, d_out, n, 1); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); // 精确计时 kernel cudaEventRecord(start); heavyCompute<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_in, d_out, n, iters); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); // D2H 传输 cudaMemcpy(h_out, d_out, size, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); cudaEventRecord(stopTotal); cudaEventSynchronize(stopTotal); float kernelMs = 0, totalMs = 0; cudaEventElapsedTime(&kernelMs, start, stop); cudaEventElapsedTime(&totalMs, startTotal, stopTotal); float h2dMs = 0, d2hMs = 0; // 单独测传输时间 cudaEventRecord(start); cudaMemcpy(d_in, h_in, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); cudaEventElapsedTime(&h2dMs, start, stop); cudaEventRecord(start); cudaMemcpy(h_out, d_out, size, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); cudaEventElapsedTime(&d2hMs, start, stop); printf(" Kernel 计算耗时: %8.3f ms\n", kernelMs); printf(" H2D 传输耗时: %8.3f ms\n", h2dMs); printf(" D2H 传输耗时: %8.3f ms\n", d2hMs); printf(" 总耗时 (含传输): %8.3f ms\n", totalMs); printf("\n"); // 性能对 // ... 点积归约

PG 10.14

PG 10.14
▶
week1_basics/day06_dot_product.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 06: 向量点积 — 理解归约 (Reduction) * ========================================= * 学习目标: * 1. 理解并行归约 (parallel reduction) 的概念 * 2. 学习使用 __syncthreads() 进行线程同步 * 3. 理解 block 内归约 + block 间归约的两阶段策略 * 4. 对比不同归约策略的性能 * * 关键概念: * - 点积需要全局归约, 但 GPU 没有全局同步原语 * - 解决方案: 每个 block 做局部归约 -> 写回全局内存 -> 最终在 CPU 或另一个 kernel 归约 * - __syncthreads() 只能同步同一个 block 内的线程 * - 共享内存用于 block 内的高效数据交换 * * 编译: nvcc day06_dot_product.cu -o day06 * 运行: ./day06 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 朴素归约 (interleaved addressing) // 每个 block 处理一段数据, 归约到 block 内, 写入部分和 // ============================================================ __global__ void dotProductNaive(const float *A, const float *B, float *partialSums, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // Step 1: 每个线程计算一个元素乘积, 载入共享内存 sdata[tid] = (idx < n) ? A[idx] * B[idx] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); // Step 2: 归约 (interleaved addressing) // 注意: 这种方式会有 bank conflict for (int stride = 1; stride < blockDim.x; stride *= 2) { if (tid % (2 * stride) == 0) { sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + stride]; } __syncthreads(); } // Step 3: block 0 的 thread 0 写入部分和 if (tid == 0) { partialSums[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; } }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Kernel 2: 改进归约 (sequential addressing) // 避免 bank conflict, 使用连续地址访问 // ============================================================ __global__ void dotProductImproved(const float *A, const float *B, float *partialSums, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; sdata[tid] = (idx < n) ? A[idx] * B[idx] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); // 使用 sequential addressing: tid 访问 sdata[tid] 和 sdata[tid + offset] for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) { if (tid < stride) { sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + stride]; } __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) { partialSums[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; } }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // Kernel 3: 最终阶段归约 (将所有 partial sum 归约为一个值) // 用一个 block 处理所有 partial sums // ============================================================ __global__ void finalReduction(float *partialSums, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; sdata[tid] = (tid < n) ? partialSums[tid] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) { if (tid < stride) { sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + stride]; } __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) { partialSums[0] = sdata[0]; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 06: 向量点积 — 并行归约\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 24; // 16M 元素 size_t size = n * sizeof(float); printf("数据量: %d 元素 (%.2f MB)\n\n", n, (float)size / 1e6); // Host 数据 float *h_A = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_B = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_A[i] = (float)(rand() % 1000) / 1000.0f; h_B[i] = (float)(rand() % 1000) / 1000.0f; } // CPU 参考计算 printf("--- CPU 参考计算 ---\n"); float cpuResult = cpuDotProduct(h_A, h_B, n); printf(" CPU 点积结果: %f\n\n", cpuResult); // Device 数据 float *d_A, *d_B; size_t partialSize = gridSize * sizeof(float); float *d_partial; cudaMalloc(&d_partial, partialSize); // --- 朴素归约 --- printf("--- 朴素归约 (interleaved) ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // 预热 dotProductNaive<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_A, d_B, d_partial, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) { dotProductNaive<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_A, d_B, d_partial, n); printf(" GPU 结果: %f\n", gpuResult); printf(" 误差: %e\n", fabs(gpuResult - cpuResult)); printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms (kernel only, 50次平均)\n", ms / 50); printf(" 吞吐: %.2f GFLOPS\n", (2.0 * n) / (ms / 50 * 1e6)); cudaEventDestroy(start); cudaEventDestroy(stop); } // --- 改进归约 --- printf("\n--- 改进归约 (sequential) ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // 预热 dotProductImproved<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_A, d_B, d_partial, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++ // ... (后续代码省略)

综合练习

PG 5.3

week1_basics/day07_week1_review.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 07: Week 1 综合练习 — CUDA 矩阵运算 * ========================================== * 本日综合运用 Week 1 学到的所有知识: * - Kernel 编写与执行配置 * - 错误处理宏 * - CUDA Event 计时 * - 共享内存初步使用 * - CPU/GPU 性能对比 * * 任务: 实现 1D 卷积 (模糊滤波) * output[i] = sum_{j=-R}^{R} input[i+j] * kernel[j] / (2R+1) * 这比向量加法更复杂, 需要处理边界和数据复用 * * 编译: nvcc day07_week1_review.cu -o day07 * 运行: ./day07 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 朴素 1D 卷积 (无共享内存) // 每个线程独立从全局内存读取数据, 有大量冗余读取 // ============================================================ __global__ void conv1dNaive(const float *input, float *output, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx >= n) return; float sum = 0.0f; for (int j = -RADIUS; j <= RADIUS; j++) { int sIdx = idx + j; // 边界处理: clamp 到 [0, n-1] sIdx = max(0, min(n - 1, sIdx)); sum += input[sIdx]; } output[idx] = sum / (2 * RADIUS + 1); }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Kernel 2: 使用共享内存的 1D 卷积 // 每个 block 协作加载一段数据到共享内存, 减少全局内存访问 // ============================================================ __global__ void conv1dShared(const float *input, float *output, int n) { // 共享内存: blockSize + 2*RADIUS (halo 区域) __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE + 2 * RADIUS]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int gid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 加载中心区域 int loadIdx = gid - RADIUS; if (loadIdx >= 0 && loadIdx < n) { sdata[tid] = input[loadIdx]; } else { sdata[tid] = 0.0f; } // 第一个 block 需要 halo (左侧) if (tid < RADIUS) { int haloIdx = gid - RADIUS - (RADIUS - tid); // 用 clamp 处理边界 haloIdx = max(0, min(n - 1, haloIdx)); // 其实是 sdata[0..RADIUS-1] 对应 input[gid-RADIUS-RADIUS+tid] } // 修正: 重新设计共享内存布局 // sdata[0 .. RADIUS-1] -> 左侧 halo // sdata[RADIUS .. RADIUS+BLOCK_SIZE-1] -> 中心数据 // sdata[RADIUS+BLOCK_SIZE ..] -> 右侧 halo // 简化: 直接加载 // 每个 thread 加载一个中心元素 int centerIdx = gid; if (centerIdx < n) sdata[RADIUS + tid] = input[centerIdx]; else sdata[RADIUS + tid] = 0.0f; // 加载左侧 halo (前 RADIUS 个线程) if (tid < RADIUS) { int lIdx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x - RADIUS + tid; sdata[tid] = (lIdx >= 0) ? input[lIdx] : input[0]; } // 加载右侧 halo (后 RADIUS 个线程) if (tid >= BLOCK_SIZE - RADIUS) { int rIdx = (blockIdx.x + 1) * blockDim.x + (tid - (BLOCK_SIZE - RADIUS)); sdata[RADIUS + BLOCK_SIZE + (tid - (BLOCK_SIZE - RADIUS))] = (rIdx < n) ? input[rIdx] : input[n - 1]; } __syncthreads(); // 计算卷积 if (gid < n) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int j = 0; j < 2 * RADIUS + 1; j++) { sum += sdata[tid + j]; } output[gid] = sum / (2 * RADIUS + 1); } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 07: Week1 综合 — 1D 卷积\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 24; // 16M size_t size = n * sizeof(float); i < n; i++) { maxErr = fmaxf(maxErr, fabsf(h_out[i] - h_ref[i])); } printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms (100次平均)\n", ms / 100); printf(" 最大误差: %e\n", maxErr); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s\n", (2.0f * size) / (ms / 100 * 1e6)); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) { conv // ... 08

共享内存

PG 6.2.4, BPG 10.2.3

PG 6.2.4, BPG 10.2.3
▶
week2_memory/day08_shared_memory.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 08: 共享内存基础 — 理解最快的 GPU 内存 * ============================================= * 学习目标: * 1. 理解 GPU 内存层次: 全局 → L2 → 共享内存 → 寄存器 * 2. 掌握 __shared__ 声明和共享内存使用模式 * 3. 理解共享内存的 bank 和 bank conflict * 4. 实现共享内存版的向量加法并对比性能 * * 内存层次 (速度从快到慢): * 寄存器 (~1 cycle) > 共享内存 (~5 cycle) > L1/L2 缓存 > 全局内存 (~400 cycle) * * 共享内存特点: * - 每个 SM 有一定量的共享内存 (通常 48KB-100KB) * - 在 block 内共享, 不同 block 之间隔离 * - 生命周期与 block 一致 * - 被组织为 32 个 bank, 同一 bank 的并发访问会产生 bank conflict * * 编译: nvcc day08_shared_memory.cu -o day08 * 运行: ./day08 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 不使用共享内存 — 每个 thread 从全局内存重复读取 // 场景: 计算 Y[i] = X[i] + X[i-1] + X[i+1] (3点平均滤波) // 每个 X[i] 被周围 3 个线程读取, 全局内存访问冗余 // ============================================================ __global__ void smoothGlobal(const float *X, float *Y, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx == 0 || idx == n - 1) return; Y[idx] = (X[idx - 1] + X[idx] + X[idx + 1]) / 3.0f; }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Kernel 2: 使用共享内存 — block 内协作加载, 减少全局内存访问 // 每个 block 加载一段数据到共享内存, 然后从共享内存读取 // ============================================================ __global__ void smoothShared(const float *X, float *Y, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE + 2]; // +2 for halo int tid = threadIdx.x; int gid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 加载中心数据 if (gid < n) { sdata[tid + 1] = X[gid]; // 偏移 1 留给左侧 halo } // 加载左侧 halo (第一个线程加载前一个 block 的最后一个元素) if (tid == 0 && gid > 0) { sdata[0] = X[gid - 1]; } else if (gid == 0) { sdata[0] = X[0]; // 边界 clamp } // 加载右侧 halo (最后一个线程加载下一个 block 的第一个元素) if (tid == BLOCK_SIZE - 1 && gid < n - 1) { sdata[BLOCK_SIZE + 1] = X[gid + 1]; } else if (gid == n - 1) { sdata[BLOCK_SIZE + 1] = X[n - 1]; // 边界 clamp } __syncthreads(); // 等待所有数据加载完成 // 从共享内存计算 (快速!) if (gid > 0 && gid < n - 1) { Y[gid] = (sdata[tid] + sdata[tid + 1] + sdata[tid + 2]) / 3.0f; } }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // 演示: 共享内存的 bank conflict // ============================================================ __global__ void bankConflictDemo(float *out) { // 32 个 bank, 每个 bank 宽度 4 bytes (32-bit) // sdata[0], sdata[32], sdata[64]... 在 bank 0 // sdata[1], sdata[33], sdata[65]... 在 bank 1 __shared__ float sdata[1024]; int tid = threadIdx.x; // 无冲突: 每个 thread 访问不同 bank // sdata[tid] -> thread 0 访问 bank 0, thread 1 访问 bank 1, ... sdata[tid] = tid * 1.0f; // 有冲突: 所有 thread 访问同一 bank // sdata[0] -> 所有 thread 访问 bank 0, 32-way conflict! // float val = sdata[0]; // 不要这样做! __syncthreads(); out[tid] = sdata[tid]; }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 08: 共享内存基础\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 22; // 4M size_t size = n * sizeof(float); float *h_X = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_Y = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_X[i] = (float)i; } float *d_X, *d_Y; cudaMalloc(&d_X, size); cudaMalloc(&d_Y, size); cudaMemcpy(d_X, h_X, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); int gridSize = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; // 测试全局内存版本 printf("--- 全局内存版本 ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); smoothGlobal<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_X, d_Y, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 1000; i++) { smoothGlobal<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_X, d_Y, n); } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); printf(" 耗时: %.4f ms (1000次平均)\n", ms / 1000); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s\n", (3.0f * size) / (ms / 1000 * 1e6)); // 3.0f 因为每个元素被读3次 (但可能被缓存) cudaEventDestroy(start); cudaEventDestroy(stop); } // 测试共享内存版本 printf("\n--- 共享内存版本 ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); smoothShared<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_X, d_Y, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 1000; i++) { smoothShared<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_X, d_Y, n); } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); printf(" 耗时: %.4f ms (1000次平均)\n", ms / 1000); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s\n", (1.0f * size) / (ms / 1000 * 1e6)); // 1.0f 因为每个元素从全局内存只读1次 cudaEventDestroy(start); cudaEventDestroy(stop); } // 验证结果 cudaMemcpy(h_Y, d_Y, size, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); bool ok = true; for (int i = 1; i < n - 1; i++) { float expected = (h_X[i-1] + h_X[i] + h_X[i+1]) / 3.0f; if (fabs(h_Y[i] - expected) > 1e-4f) { ok = false; break; } } printf("\n 验证: %s\n", ok ? "✓ 通过" : "✗ 失败"); // 打印共享内存信息 cudaDeviceProp prop; cudaGetDeviceProperties(&prop, 0); printf("\n--- 设备共享内存信 // ... (后续代码省略)
编译 & 运行

week2_memory/day09_matrix_transpose.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 09: 矩阵转置 — 共享内存经典案例 * ===================================== * 学习目标: * 1. 矩阵转置是共享内存的教科书级应用 * 2. 理解全局内存合并访问 (coalesced access) 的重要性 * 3. 理解为什么朴素转置慢 (写不合并) * 4. 用共享内存实现合并读写 * * 核心问题: * 朴素转置: 读行(合并) → 写列(不合并, stride=N) * 共享内存转置: 读行(合并) → 写共享内存 → 读共享内存(转置) → 写行(合并) * * 编译: nvcc day09_matrix_transpose.cu -o day09 * 运行: ./day09 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 朴素矩阵转置 (只用全局内存) // 读: 合并 (连续 thread 读连续列) // 写: 不合并 (连续 thread 写到 stride=N 的位置) // ============================================================ __global__ void transposeNaive(const float *input, float *output, int n) { int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; // 列 int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; // 行 if (x < n && y < n) { // output[x][y] = input[y][x] 即 output[x*n+y] = input[y*n+x] output[x * n + y] = input[y * n + x]; } }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Kernel 2: 共享内存矩阵转置 // 关键: 通过共享内存实现 "读行写行" 的合并模式 // ============================================================ __global__ void transposeShared(const float *input, float *output, int n) { __shared__ float tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1]; // +1 消除 bank conflict! // 坐标计算 int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; // 从全局内存读取 (合并访问) 到共享内存 if (x < n && y < n) { tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = input[y * n + x]; } __syncthreads(); // 转置后的坐标: 交换 blockIdx x = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; // 注意: blockIdx.y y = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; // 注意: blockIdx.x // 从共享内存读取 (转置读) 写入全局内存 (合并访问) if (x < n && y < n) { output[y * n + x] = tile[threadIdx.x][threadIdx.y]; // 交换 x,y } }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // Kernel 3: 对角线优化转置 // 处理非方阵或进一步优化时, 可以按对角线遍历 tile // 这里演示基本原理 // ============================================================ __global__ void transposeDiagonal(const float *input, float *output, int n) { __shared__ float tile[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE + 1]; // 处理对角线 block (blockIdx.x == blockIdx.y) // 非对角线 block 需要 swap int blockIdx_x, blockIdx_y; if (gridDim.x == gridDim.y) { blockIdx_y = blockIdx.x; blockIdx_x = (blockIdx.x + blockIdx.y) % gridDim.x; } else { blockIdx_y = blockIdx.x; blockIdx_x = blockIdx.y; } int x = blockIdx_x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx_y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; if (x < n && y < n) { tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = input[y * n + x]; } __syncthreads(); x = blockIdx_y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; y = blockIdx_x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; if (x < n && y < n) { output[y * n + x] = tile[threadIdx.x][threadIdx.y]; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 09: 矩阵转置\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); // 使用较大的方阵 int n = 4096; size_t size = (size_t)n * n * sizeof(float); printf("矩阵: %d x %d (%.2f MB)\n\n", n, n, (float)size / 1e6); float *h_in = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_out = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n * n; i++) { h_in[i] = (float)i; } float *d_in, *d_out; cudaMalloc(&d_in, size); cudaMalloc(&d_out, size); cudaMemcpy(d_in, h_in, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); dim3 blockSize(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE); dim3 gridSize((n + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE, (n + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE); // --- 朴素版本 --- printf("--- 朴素转置 (全局内存) ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); transposeNaive<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_in, d_out, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) { transposeNaive<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_in, d_out, n); } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms (100次平均)\n", ms / 100); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s (理论峰值需要看设备)\n", (2.0f * size) / (ms / 100 * 1e6)); cudaEventDestroy(start); cudaEventDestroy(stop); } // --- 共享内存版本 --- printf("\n--- 共享内存转置 ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); transposeShared<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_in, d_out, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) { transposeShared<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_in, d_out, n); } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms (100次平均)\n", ms / 100); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s\n", (2.0f * size) / (ms / 100 * 1e6)); cudaEventDestroy(start); cudaEventDestroy(stop); } // --- 对角线优化版本 --- printf("\n--- 对角线优化转置 ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); transposeDiagonal<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_in, d_out, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); // ... (后续代码省略)

常量内存

week2_memory/day10_constant_memory.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 10: 常量内存 — 优化只读数据访问 * ======================================= * 学习目标: * 1. 理解常量内存的特点和使用场景 * 2. 掌握 __constant__ 声明和 cudaMemcpyToSymbol * 3. 理解常量缓存 (constant cache) 的工作原理 * 4. 对比常量内存 vs 全局内存 vs 共享内存的性能 * * 常量内存特点: * - 总共 64KB, 所有 SM 共享 * - 只读 (device 代码不能修改) * - 有专用缓存, 延迟类似共享内存 (~5 cycle) * - 广播机制: 当一个 warp 中所有线程读同一地址时, 只需 1 次访问 * - 如果 warp 中不同线程读不同地址, 会串行化 (比全局内存还慢!) * * 最佳场景: 所有线程读取相同的少量只读数据 * 典型用途: 卷积核、变换矩阵、查找表 * * 编译: nvcc day10_constant_memory.cu -o day10 * 运行: ./day10 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 使用常量内存的 2D 变换 // 每个线程读取相同的变换矩阵 → 广播, 高效 // ============================================================ __global__ void transform2D(const float *input, float *output, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx * 2 + 1 >= n) return; float x = input[idx * 2]; float y = input[idx * 2 + 1]; // 所有线程读取相同的 c_transform → 常量缓存广播 // c_transform[0], c_transform[1], c_transform[2], c_transform[3] output[idx * 2] = c_transform[0] * x + c_transform[1] * y; output[idx * 2 + 1] = c_transform[2] * x + c_transform[3] * y; }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Kernel 2: 使用全局内存的 2D 变换 (对比用) // ============================================================ __global__ void transform2DGlobal(const float *input, float *output, const float *transform, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx * 2 + 1 >= n) return; float x = input[idx * 2]; float y = input[idx * 2 + 1]; output[idx * 2] = transform[0] * x + transform[1] * y; output[idx * 2 + 1] = transform[2] * x + transform[3] * y; }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // Kernel 3: 使用常量内存的 FIR 滤波器 // 卷积核放在常量内存, 所有线程共享 // ============================================================ __global__ void firFilter(const float *input, float *output, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx >= n) return; float sum = 0.0f; for (int i = 0; i < MASK_SIZE; i++) { int sIdx = idx + i - MASK_SIZE / 2; sIdx = max(0, min(n - 1, sIdx)); // c_mask[i] 被 warp 中所有线程同时读取 → 广播 sum += input[sIdx] * c_mask[i]; } output[idx] = sum; }
Kernel 4
// ============================================================ // Kernel 4: 使用全局内存的 FIR 滤波器 (对比用) // ============================================================ __global__ void firFilterGlobal(const float *input, float *output, const float *mask, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx >= n) return; float sum = 0.0f; for (int i = 0; i < MASK_SIZE; i++) { int sIdx = idx + i - MASK_SIZE / 2; sIdx = max(0, min(n - 1, sIdx)); sum += input[sIdx] * mask[i]; } output[idx] = sum; }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 10: 常量内存\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 22; size_t size = n * sizeof(float); float *h_input = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_output = (float *)malloc(size); float h_mask[MASK_SIZE]; // 生成高斯滤波核 for (int i = 0; i < MASK_SIZE; i++) { float x = (float)(i - MASK_SIZE / 2); h_mask[i] = expf(-x * x / 8.0f); } // 归一化 float maskSum = 0; for (int i = 0; i < MASK_SIZE; i++) maskSum += h_mask[i]; for (int i = 0; i < MASK_SIZE; i++) h_mask[i] /= maskSum; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_input[i] = sinf(i * 0.01f) + (rand() % 100 - 50) * 0.01f; } // 变换矩阵 (旋转 30 度) float h_transform[4] = { 0.866f, -0.5f, // cos(30), -sin(30) 0.5f, 0.866f // sin(30), cos(30) }; float *d_input, *d_output, *d_mask, *d_transform; cudaMalloc(&d_input, size); cudaMalloc(&d_output, size); cudaMalloc(&d_mask, MASK_SIZE * sizeof(float)); cudaMalloc(&d_transform, 4 * sizeof(float)); cudaMemcpy(d_input, h_input, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); cudaMemcpy(d_mask, h_mask, MASK_SIZE * sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); cudaMemcpy(d_transform, h_transform, 4 * sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); // 拷贝到常量内存 cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_mask, h_mask, MASK_SIZE * sizeof(float)); cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_transform, h_transform, 4 * sizeof(float)); int gridSize = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; // --- FIR 滤波器对比 --- printf("--- FIR 滤波器 (%d tap) ---\n\n", MASK_SIZE); printf(" 常量内存版本:\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); firFilter<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_output, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 200; i++) firFilter<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_output, n);
编译 & 运行
# 编译 nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 week2_memory/day10_constant_memory.cu -o week2_memory/day10_constant_memory # 运行 ./week2_memory/day10_constant_memory

纹理内存

week2_memory/day11_texture_memory.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 11: 纹理内存 — 空间局部性优化 * ==================================== * 学习目标: * 1. 理解纹理内存的特点和适用场景 * 2. 掌握 CUDA 纹理对象的创建和使用 * 3. 理解纹理缓存的 2D 空间局部性 * 4. 使用纹理内存的硬件特性: 自动边界处理、插值 * * 纹理内存特点: * - 有专用的 2D 空间缓存, 适合有空间局部性的访问模式 * - 支持硬件插值 (线性插值免费!) * - 支持自动边界处理 (clamp, wrap, mirror) * - 适合图像处理、有限元、 stencil 计算 * * 现代 API: 使用 cudaTextureObject_t (CUDA 3.x+) * 旧 API: __device__ __bind_to_texture__ 已弃用 * * 编译: nvcc day11_texture_memory.cu -o day11 * 运行: ./day11 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 使用纹理内存的 2D stencil 计算 // 利用纹理缓存的 2D 空间局部性 // ============================================================ __global__ void stencil2DTexture(cudaTextureObject_t tex, float *output, int width, int height) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; if (x >= width || y >= height) return; // 5 点 stencil: center + up + down + left + right // tex2D 自动处理边界 (默认 clamp to edge) float center = tex2D<float>(tex, x, y); float up = tex2D<float>(tex, x, y - 1); float down = tex2D<float>(tex, x, y + 1); float left = tex2D<float>(tex, x - 1, y); float right = tex2D<float>(tex, x + 1, y); output[y * width + x] = (center + up + down + left + right) * 0.2f; }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Kernel 2: 全局内存版本 (对比用) // ============================================================ __global__ void stencil2DGlobal(const float *input, float *output, int width, int height) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; if (x >= width || y >= height) return; float center = input[y * width + x]; float up = (y > 0) ? input[(y - 1) * width + x] : center; float down = (y < height - 1) ? input[(y + 1) * width + x] : center; float left = (x > 0) ? input[y * width + x - 1] : center; float right = (x < width - 1) ? input[y * width + x + 1] : center; output[y * width + x] = (center + up + down + left + right) * 0.2f; }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // Kernel 3: 使用纹理的硬件插值 // 对非整数坐标, tex2D 自动做双线性插值 // ============================================================ __global__ void bilinearSample(cudaTextureObject_t tex, float *output, int width, int height) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx >= width * height) return; // 生成螺旋采样路径 int x = idx % width; int y = idx / width; float cx = width / 2.0f; float cy = height / 2.0f; float r = 50.0f + (float)(idx % 100) * 0.5f; float angle = (float)idx * 0.01f; float sx = cx + r * cosf(angle); float sy = cy + r * sinf(angle); // tex2D 自动做双线性插值! output[idx] = tex2D<float>(tex, sx, sy); }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 11: 纹理内存\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int width = 4096; int height = 4096; size_t size = (size_t)width * height * sizeof(float); printf("图像: %d x %d (%.2f MB)\n\n", width, height, (float)size / 1e6); float *h_input = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_output = (float *)malloc(size); for (int y = 0; y < height; y++) { for (int x = 0; x < width; x++) { h_input[y * width + x] = sinf(x * 0.01f) * cosf(y * 0.01f) * 100.0f; } } float *d_input, *d_output; cudaMalloc(&d_input, size); cudaMalloc(&d_output, size); cudaMemcpy(d_input, h_input, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); // 创建纹理对象 cudaTextureObject_t texObj = createTexture2D(h_input, width, height); dim3 blockSize(16, 16); dim3 gridSize((width + 15) / 16, (height + 15) / 16); // --- 纹理内存版本 --- printf("--- 2D Stencil (纹理内存) ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); stencil2DTexture<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(texObj, d_output, width, height); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) { stencil2DTexture<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(texObj, d_output, width, height); } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms (50次平均)\n", ms / 50); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s\n", (2.0f * size) / (ms / 50 * 1e6)); cudaEventDestroy(start); cudaEventDestroy(stop); } // --- 全局内存版本 --- printf("\n--- 2D Stencil (全局内存) ---\n"); { cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); stencil2DGlobal<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_input, d_output, width, height); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) { stencil2DGlobal<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_input, d_output, width, height); } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms (50次平均)\n", ms / 50); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s\n", (2.0f * size) / (ms / 50 * 1e6)); cudaEventDestroy(start); cudaEventDestroy(stop); } // ... (后续代码省略)

合并访问

BPG 10.2.1

BPG 10.2.1
▶
week2_memory/day12_global_coalescing.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 12: 全局内存合并访问 — GPU 性能的关键 * ============================================= * 学习目标: * 1. 深入理解合并访问 (coalesced access) 的原理 * 2. 理解为什么非合并访问会慢 10-30 倍 * 3. 学会识别和修复非合并访问模式 * 4. 对比 AoS vs SoA 数据布局的性能差异 * * 关键概念: * - 一个 warp (32 threads) 同时执行 * - 合并访问: warp 中连续 threads 访问连续地址 → 1 次内存事务 * - 非合并访问: warp 中 threads 访问非连续地址 → 多次内存事务 * - 最差情况: stride 访问 → 32 次事务 * * 编译: nvcc day12_global_coalescing.cu -o day12 * 运行: ./day12 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 合并访问 — 连续线程读连续地址 // thread 0 读 array[0], thread 1 读 array[1], ... // ============================================================ __global__ void coalescedRead(const float *input, float *output, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { output[idx] = input[idx] * 2.0f; } }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Kernel 2: 非合并访问 — stride 访问 // thread 0 读 array[0], thread 1 读 array[STRIDE], ... // 这模拟了常见的非合并访问模式 // ============================================================ __global__ void stridedRead(const float *input, float *output, int n, int stride) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int accessIdx = idx * stride; if (accessIdx < n) { output[idx] = input[accessIdx] * 2.0f; } }
Kernel 3
__global__ void processAoS(const float3_aos *input, float3_aos *output, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { // 只需要 x, 但整个 struct 被加载 (warp 中每3个float只用到1个) output[idx].x = input[idx].x * 2.0f; output[idx].y = input[idx].y; output[idx].z = input[idx].z; } }
Kernel 4
__global__ void processSoA(float3_soa input, float3_soa output, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { // 访问 x[] 完全合并 output.x[idx] = input.x[idx] * 2.0f; output.y[idx] = input.y[idx]; output.z[idx] = input.z[idx]; } }
Kernel 5
// ============================================================ // Kernel 5: 转置读取模式 (通过共享内存修复非合并) // 原始模式: warp 中各线程读跨行 (stride=N), 非合并 // 优化: 先合并读入共享内存, 再转置读取 // ============================================================ __global__ void matrixReadShared(const float *matrix, float *colSums, int rows, int cols) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE * BLOCK_SIZE]; // 简化: 假设 BLOCK_SIZE tile int tx = threadIdx.x; int ty = threadIdx.y; int row = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_SIZE + ty; int col = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + tx; // 合并读: 每 thread 读一个元素 if (row < rows && col < cols) { sdata[ty * BLOCK_SIZE + tx] = matrix[row * cols + col]; } else { sdata[ty * BLOCK_SIZE + tx] = 0.0f; } __syncthreads(); // 沿列方向求和 (每个 thread 对应一列) // 注意: 这里 sdata[tx * BLOCK_SIZE + ty] 是转置读 if (tx < BLOCK_SIZE && ty == 0) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int i = 0; i < BLOCK_SIZE; i++) { sum += sdata[i * BLOCK_SIZE + tx]; } // 累加到全局结果 atomicAdd(&colSums[blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + tx], sum); } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 12: 全局内存合并访问\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 22; // 4M size_t size = n * sizeof(float); float *h_data = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) h_data[i] = (float)i; float *d_in, *d_out; cudaMalloc(&d_in, size); cudaMalloc(&d_out, size); cudaMemcpy(d_in, h_data, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); int gridSize = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // --- 合并访问 --- printf("--- 合并访问 (stride=1) ---\n"); { coalescedRead<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_in, d_out, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 500; i++) coalescedRead<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_in, d_out, n); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); printf(" 耗时: %.4f ms\n", ms / 500); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s\n\n", (2.0f * size) / (ms / 500 * 1e6)); } // --- 不同 stride 的非合并访问 --- int strides[] = {2, 4, 8, 16, 32}; for (int s = 0; s < 5; s++) { int stride = strides[s]; int effectiveN = n / stride; int gs = (effectiveN + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; stridedRead<<<gs, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_in, d_out, n, stride); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 500; i++) stridedRead<<<gs, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_in, d_out, n, stride); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); printf("--- 非合并访问 (stride=%2d) ---\n", stride); printf(" 耗时: %.4f ms\n", ms / 500); printf(" 有效带宽: %.2f GB/s\n", (2.0f * effectiveN * sizeof(float)) / (ms / 500 * 1e6)); printf(" 速度降低: %.1fx\n\n", (ms / 500) / ((2.0f * size) / (ms / 500 * 1e6) * 0 + 0.001)); } // --- AoS vs SoA --- printf("--- AoS vs SoA (float3 结构体) ---\n\n");

学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 13: 固定内存 — 加速 Host-Device 数据传输 * ================================================ * 学习目标: * 1. 理解可分页内存 vs 固定内存 (pinned memory) 的区别 * 2. 掌握 cudaMallocHost / cudaFreeHost 的使用 * 3. 理解为什么固定内存能加速传输 * 4. 学习异步传输 (cudaMemcpyAsync) 和固定内存的配合 * * 关键概念: * - 普通malloc分配的是可分页内存, OS可以将其换出到磁盘 * - GPU不能直接访问可分页内存, 需要先拷贝到暂存区 (staging buffer) * - 固定内存不可换出, GPU可以直接DMA访问, 省去中间拷贝 * - 固定内存传输速度可达满带宽, 可分页内存通常只有一半 * * 注意: * - 固定内存不能分配太多 (会减少系统可用内存) * - 过度使用固定内存会降低整体系统性能 * - 适合频繁传输的场景 * * 编译: nvcc day13_pinned_memory.cu -o day13 * 运行: ./day13 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
__global__ void vectorScale(const float *in, float *out, float alpha, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { out[idx] = in[idx] * alpha; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)

分块矩阵乘法

BPG 10.2.3

BPG 10.2.3
▶
week2_memory/day14_week2_review.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 14: Week 2 综合 — 分块矩阵乘法 * ===================================== * 本日综合运用 Week 2 所学内存知识: * - 共享内存 (tiled 矩阵乘法) * - 全局内存合并访问 * - 内存层次优化 * * 矩阵乘法 C = A * B (A: MxK, B: KxN, C: MxN) * 朴素版本: 每个元素需要 K 次全局内存读取 * 分块版本: 使用共享内存 tile, 大幅减少全局内存访问 * * 这是 CUDA 编程最重要的模式之一! * * 编译: nvcc day14_week2_review.cu -o day14 * 运行: ./day14 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Kernel 1: 朴素矩阵乘法 (全局内存) // 每个 thread 计算一个 C 元素, 从全局内存读 A 行和 B 列 // 问题: A 和 B 的每个元素被重复读取很多次 // ============================================================ __global__ void matMulNaive(const float *A, const float *B, float *C, int M, int N, int K) { int row = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; int col = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (row < M && col < N) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int i = 0; i < K; i++) { sum += A[row * K + i] * B[i * N + col]; } C[row * N + col] = sum; } }
Kernel 2
* 核心思想: 将大矩阵分成 TILE_SIZE x TILE_SIZE 的小块 * 每次将一个 A tile 和 B tile 加载到共享内存 * 在共享内存中完成乘加, 大幅减少全局内存访问 * * 内存访问对比 (K=1024, TILE=32): * 朴素: A 每元素被读 N 次, B 每元素被读 M 次 * 分块: A 每元素被读 N/TILE 次, B 每元素被读 M/TILE 次 * → 全局内存访问减少 TILE 倍! // ============================================================ __global__ void matMulTiled(const float *A, const float *B, float *C, int M, int N, int K) { // 声明共享内存 tile __shared__ float s_A[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE]; __shared__ float s_B[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE]; int row = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; int col = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; float sum = 0.0f; // 遍历 K 维度的所有 tile int numTiles = (K + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE; for (int t = 0; t < numTiles; t++) { // --- 1. 协作加载 A 和 B 的 tile 到共享内存 --- // A tile: 从全局内存读取 (合并访问) int aRow = row; int aCol = t * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; s_A[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = (aRow < M && aCol < K) ? A[aRow * K + aCol] : 0.0f; // B tile: 从全局内存读取 (合并访问) int bRow = t * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; int bCol = col; s_B[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = (bRow < K && bCol < N) ? B[bRow * N + bCol] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); // 等待 tile 加载完成 // --- 2. 在共享内存中计算部分和 --- for (int i = 0; i < TILE_SIZE; i++) { sum += s_A[threadIdx.y][i] * s_B[i][threadIdx.x]; } __syncthreads(); // 确保计算完成后再加载下一个 tile } // --- 3. 写回结果 --- if (row < M && col < N) { C[row * N + col] = sum; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 14: Week2 综合 — 分块矩阵乘法\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int M = 1024, N = 1024, K = 1024; size_t sizeA = (size_t)M * K * sizeof(float); size_t sizeB = (size_t)K * N * sizeof(float); size_t sizeC = (size_t)M * N * sizeof(float); printf("矩阵: A[%dx%d] * B[%dx%d] = C[%dx%d]\n", M, K, K, N, M, N); printf("数据量: A=%.2fMB, B=%.2fMB, C=%.2fMB\n\n", (float)sizeA/1e6, (float)sizeB/1e6, (float)sizeC/1e6); float *h_A = (float *)malloc(sizeA); float *h_B = (float *)malloc(sizeB); float *h_C = (float *)malloc(sizeC); float *h_ref = (float *)malloc(sizeC); for (int i = 0; i < M * K; i++) h_A[i] = (float)(rand() % 100) / 100.0f; for (int i = 0; i < K * N; i++) h_B[i] = (float)(rand() % 100) / 100.0f; // CPU 参考 (小规模验证) printf("--- CPU 参考计算 ---\n"); cudaMemcpy(d_B, h_B, sizeB, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); dim3 blockSize(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE); dim3 gridSize((N + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE, (M + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE); cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // 朴素版本 printf("--- 朴素矩阵乘法 ---\n"); { matMulNaive<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) matMulNaive<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); cudaMemcpy(h_C, d_C, sizeC, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); float maxErr = 0; for (int i = 0; i < M * N; i++) maxErr = fmaxf(maxErr, fabsf(h_C[i] - h_ref[i])); float gflops = (2.0f * M * N * K) / (ms / 10 * 1e6); printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms (10次平均)\n", ms / 10); printf(" 性能: %.2f GFLOPS\n", gflops); printf(" 最大误差: %e\n\n", maxErr); } // 分块版本 printf("--- 分块矩阵乘法 (共享内存) ---\n"); { matMulTiled<<<gridSize, blockSize>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = // ... 15

归约优化

BPG 12

BPG 12
▶
week3_patterns/day15_reduction.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 15: 并行归约 — 从朴素到最优 * ================================= * 学习目标: * 1. 深入理解并行归约的多种策略 * 2. 对比 interleaved vs sequential addressing * 3. 理解 warp divergence 对性能的影响 * 4. 学习 warp 级归约优化 (最后 32 个线程无需同步) * * 归约是并行计算的基础模式: * sum, max, min, product 等都需要归约 * 性能差异可达 5-10 倍! * * 编译: nvcc day15_reduction.cu -o day15 * 运行: ./day15 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // V1: 朴素归约 — interleaved addressing // 问题: tid % (2*stride) 导致大量 warp divergence // ============================================================ __global__ void reduceV1(const float *input, float *output, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; sdata[tid] = (idx < n) ? input[idx] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); for (int stride = 1; stride < blockDim.x; stride *= 2) { if (tid % (2 * stride) == 0) { sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + stride]; } __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) output[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // V2: 改进 — sequential addressing // 消除了 tid % 运算, 减少 divergence // ============================================================ __global__ void reduceV2(const float *input, float *output, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; sdata[tid] = (idx < n) ? input[idx] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) { if (tid < stride) { sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + stride]; } __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) output[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // V3: 第一次加法在加载时完成 (减少数据量) // 每个 thread 先从全局内存读两个元素相加, 再存入共享内存 // 相当于 block 处理的数据量翻倍 // ============================================================ __global__ void reduceV3(const float *input, float *output, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * (BLOCK_SIZE * 2) + threadIdx.x; // 加载时做第一次加法 float val = 0.0f; if (idx < n) val += input[idx]; if (idx + BLOCK_SIZE < n) val += input[idx + BLOCK_SIZE]; sdata[tid] = val; __syncthreads(); for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) { if (tid < stride) { sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + stride]; } __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) output[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; }
Kernel 4
__global__ void reduceV4(const float *input, float *output, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * (BLOCK_SIZE * 2) + threadIdx.x; float val = 0.0f; if (idx < n) val += input[idx]; if (idx + BLOCK_SIZE < n) val += input[idx + BLOCK_SIZE]; sdata[tid] = val; __syncthreads(); for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 32; stride >>= 1) { if (tid < stride) { sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + stride]; } __syncthreads(); } // 最后一个 warp 的归约 if (tid < 32) { warpReduce(sdata, tid); } if (tid == 0) output[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; }
Kernel 5
// 最终归约 kernel __global__ void finalReduce(float *data, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; sdata[tid] = (tid < n) ? data[tid] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) { if (tid < stride) sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + stride]; __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) data[0] = sdata[0]; }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 15: 并行归约 — 4种策略对比\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 24; // 16M size_t size = n * sizeof(float); printf("数据量: %d 元素 (%.2f MB)\n\n", n, (float)size / 1e6); float *h_input = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) h_input[i] = (float)(rand() % 1000) / 1000.0f; // CPU 参考 double cpuSum = 0; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) cpuSum += h_input[i]; printf("CPU 参考结果: %f\n\n", (float)cpuSum); float *d_input, *d_partial; cudaMalloc(&d_input, size); cudaMemcpy(d_input, h_input, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); int gridSize = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; cudaMalloc(&d_partial, gridSize * sizeof(float)); cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // 测试各版本 const char* names[] = {"V1 朴素 (interleaved)", "V2 改进 (sequential)", "V3 加载时加法", "V4 Warp归约优化"}; for (int v = 1; v <= 4; v++) { printf("--- %s ---\n", names[v-1]); // 预热 switch(v) { case 1: reduceV1<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_partial, n); break; case 2: reduceV2<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_partial, n); break; case 3: reduceV3<<<gridSize/2, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_partial, n); break; case 4: reduceV4<<<gridSize/2, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_partial, n); break; } cudaDeviceSynchronize(); int gs = (v <= 2) ? gridSize : gridSize / 2; cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) { switch(v) { case 1: reduceV1<<<gs, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_partial, n); break; case 2: reduceV2<<<gs, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_partial, n); break; case 3: reduceV3<<<gs, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_partial, n); break; case 4: reduceV4<<<gs, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_partial, n); break; } } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); finalReduce<<<1, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_partial, gs); float gpuSum; cudaMemcpy(&gpuSum, d_partial, sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); printf(" GPU 结果: %f\n", gpuSum); printf(" 误差: %e\n", fabsf(gpuSum - (float)cpuSum)); printf(" 耗时: %.4f ms (100次平均)\n", ms / 100); // ... (后续代码省略)

16

前缀和

PG 10.22

PG 10.22
▶
week3_patterns/day16_prefix_sum.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 16: 前缀和 (Scan) — 并行计算的核心算法 * ============================================ * 学习目标: * 1. 理解前缀和 (prefix sum / scan) 的定义和重要性 * 2. 实现 Blelloch scan (work-efficient, up-sweep + down-sweep) * 3. 理解 inclusive vs exclusive scan * 4. 学习 block scan + global scan 的两阶段策略 * * 前缀和定义: * exclusive: out[i] = in[0] + in[1] + ... + in[i-1], out[0] = 0 * inclusive: out[i] = in[0] + in[1] + ... + in[i], out[0] = in[0] * * 应用场景: * - compact (过滤): 需要计算每个元素的输出位置 * - radix sort: 统计每个桶中元素的数量 * - 流压缩、去重 * * 编译: nvcc day16_prefix_sum.cu -o day16 * 运行: ./day16 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // V1: 朴素 inclusive scan (Hillis-Steele) // 每步 stride 翻倍, 总共 log2(N) 步 // work: O(N log N), step: O(log N) // 适合: 追求最少步骤数, work 不高效 // ============================================================ __global__ void scanInclusiveNaive(const float *input, float *output, float *blockSums, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE * 2]; // 双缓冲 int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 加载数据 sdata[tid] = (idx < n) ? input[idx] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); // Hillis-Steele scan int pout = 0, pin = 1; for (int stride = 1; stride < BLOCK_SIZE; stride *= 2) { pout = 1 - pout; pin = 1 - pout; if (tid >= stride) { sdata[pout * BLOCK_SIZE + tid] = sdata[pin * BLOCK_SIZE + tid] + sdata[pin * BLOCK_SIZE + tid - stride]; } else { sdata[pout * BLOCK_SIZE + tid] = sdata[pin * BLOCK_SIZE + tid]; } __syncthreads(); } // 写出结果 if (idx < n) { output[idx] = sdata[pout * BLOCK_SIZE + tid]; } // block 的总和用于全局 scan if (tid == BLOCK_SIZE - 1) { blockSums[blockIdx.x] = sdata[pout * BLOCK_SIZE + tid]; } }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // V2: Blelloch scan (work-efficient, exclusive) // up-sweep (归约) + down-sweep (分发) // work: O(N), step: O(log N) // 更高效但实现更复杂 // ============================================================ __global__ void scanExclusiveBlelloch(const float *input, float *output, float *blockSums, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 加载 sdata[tid] = (idx < n) ? input[idx] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); // --- Up-sweep (reduction phase) --- int stride = 1; while (stride < BLOCK_SIZE) { int index = (tid + 1) * stride * 2 - 1; if (index < BLOCK_SIZE) { sdata[index] += sdata[index - stride]; } stride *= 2; __syncthreads(); } // 保存 block sum, 清零最后一个元素 if (tid == 0) { blockSums[blockIdx.x] = sdata[BLOCK_SIZE - 1]; sdata[BLOCK_SIZE - 1] = 0.0f; } __syncthreads(); // --- Down-sweep (distribution phase) --- stride = BLOCK_SIZE / 2; while (stride > 0) { int index = (tid + 1) * stride * 2 - 1; if (index < BLOCK_SIZE) { float tmp = sdata[index - stride]; sdata[index - stride] = sdata[index]; sdata[index] += tmp; } stride >>= 1; __syncthreads(); } // 写出 if (idx < n) { output[idx] = sdata[tid]; } }
Kernel 3
// 给每个 block 的结果加上前面所有 block 的和 __global__ void addBlockSums(float *output, const float *blockSums, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n && blockIdx.x > 0) { // 累加前面所有 block 的和 float prefix = 0.0f; for (int i = 0; i < blockIdx.x; i++) { prefix += blockSums[i]; } output[idx] += prefix; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 16: 前缀和 (Scan)\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 22; // 4M size_t size = n * sizeof(float); printf("数据量: %d 元素\n\n", n); float *h_input = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_output = (float *)malloc(size); float *h_ref = (float *)malloc(size); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) h_input[i] = 1.0f; // 全1, 方便验证 scanCPU(h_input, h_ref, n); printf("CPU 参考: scan[%d] = %f (期望 %d)\n\n", n - 1, h_ref[n - 1], n - 1); float *d_input, *d_output, *d_blockSums; cudaMalloc(&d_input, size); cudaMalloc(&d_output, size); cudaMemcpy(d_input, h_input, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); int gridSize = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; cudaMalloc(&d_blockSums, gridSize * sizeof(float)); cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // --- Hillis-Steele --- printf("--- Hillis-Steele (inclusive, 朴素) ---\n"); { scanInclusiveNaive<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_output, d_blockSums, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) scanInclusiveNaive<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_output, d_blockSums, n); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); // 加上 block sums addBlockSums<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_output, d_blockSums, n); cudaMemcpy(h_output, d_output, size, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); bool ok = true; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { // inclusive scan of all 1s should give i+1 if (fabs(h_output[i] - (i + 1)) > 1e-3f) { ok = false; break; } } printf(" 耗时: %.4f ms (50次平均, kernel only)\n", ms / 50); printf(" 验证: %s\n\n", ok ? "✓ 通过" : "✗ 失败"); } // --- Blelloch --- printf("--- Blelloch (exclusive, work-efficient) ---\n"); { scanExclusiveBlelloch<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_output, d_blockSums, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++) scanExclusiveBlelloch<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_output, d_blockSums, n); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); // 加上 block // ... (后续代码省略)

矩阵乘法进阶

BPG 10.2.3

BPG 10.2.3
▶
week3_patterns/day17_tiled_matmul.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 17: 分块矩阵乘法进阶 — 1D tile 和寄存器优化 * ================================================== * 学习目标: * 1. 在 Day14 基础上深入优化矩阵乘法 * 2. 使用寄存器 tile (每个线程计算多个输出元素) * 3. 理解计算强度 (arithmetic intensity) 的概念 * 4. 对比不同 tile 大小和策略的性能 * * 优化思路: * - 朴素: 每个 thread 做 1 个 C 元素, 读 K 对 A,B * - Tiled: block 共享 tile, 减少 global 访问 * - 寄存器 tile: 每个 thread 做 RM*RN 个 C 元素, 更高效利用寄存器 * * 计算强度 = FLOPS / Bytes * 矩阵乘法的计算强度可以通过 tiling 不断提高 * 当计算强度 > GPU 的计算/带宽比时, 达到计算受限 * * 编译: nvcc day17_tiled_matmul.cu -o day17 * 运行: ./day17 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // V1: 基础分块 (Day14 的版本, 作为基准) // ============================================================ __global__ void matMulTiled(const float *A, const float *B, float *C, int M, int N, int K) { __shared__ float s_A[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE]; __shared__ float s_B[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE]; int row = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; int col = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; float sum = 0.0f; for (int t = 0; t < (K + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE; t++) { s_A[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = (row < M && t * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x < K) ? A[row * K + t * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x] : 0.0f; s_B[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = (t * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y < K && col < N) ? B[(t * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y) * N + col] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); for (int i = 0; i < TILE_SIZE; i++) { sum += s_A[threadIdx.y][i] * s_B[i][threadIdx.x]; } __syncthreads(); } if (row < M && col < N) C[row * N + col] = sum; }
Kernel 2
__global__ void matMulRegTile(const float *A, const float *B, float *C, int M, int N, int K) { __shared__ float s_A[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE]; __shared__ float s_B[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE]; int tx = threadIdx.x; // 0..15 int ty = threadIdx.y; // 0..15 int rowBase = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + ty * RM; int colBase = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + tx * RN; // 4 个寄存器存 2x2 结果 float sum00 = 0.0f, sum01 = 0.0f, sum10 = 0.0f, sum11 = 0.0f; for (int t = 0; t < (K + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE; t++) { // 加载 A tile (每个线程加载 RM 个元素) for (int r = 0; r < RM; r++) { int rRow = ty * RM + r; int gRow = rowBase + r; int gCol = t * TILE_SIZE + tx; s_A[rRow][tx] = (gRow < M && gCol < K) ? A[gRow * K + gCol] : 0.0f; } // 加载 B tile (每个线程加载 RN 个元素) for (int c = 0; c < RN; c++) { int rCol = tx * RN + c; int gRow = t * TILE_SIZE + ty; int gCol = colBase + c; s_B[ty][rCol] = (gRow < K && gCol < N) ? B[gRow * N + gCol] : 0.0f; } __syncthreads(); // 计算 2x2 的部分和 for (int i = 0; i < TILE_SIZE; i++) { float a0 = s_A[ty * RM][i]; float a1 = s_A[ty * RM + 1][i]; float b0 = s_B[i][tx * RN]; float b1 = s_B[i][tx * RN + 1]; sum00 += a0 * b0; sum01 += a0 * b1; sum10 += a1 * b0; sum11 += a1 * b1; } __syncthreads(); } // 写回 if (rowBase < M && colBase < N) { C[rowBase * N + colBase] = sum00; if (colBase + 1 < N) C[rowBase * N + colBase + 1] = sum01; if (rowBase + 1 < M) C[(rowBase + 1) * N + colBase] = sum10; if (rowBase + 1 < M && colBase + 1 < N) C[(rowBase + 1) * N + colBase + 1] = sum11; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 17: 分块矩阵乘法进阶\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int M = 2048, N = 2048, K = 2048; size_t sizeA = (size_t)M * K * sizeof(float); size_t sizeB = (size_t)K * N * sizeof(float); size_t sizeC = (size_t)M * N * sizeof(float); printf("矩阵: %dx%d * %dx%d = %dx%d\n\n", M, K, K, N, M, N); float *h_A = (float *)malloc(sizeA); float *h_B = (float *)malloc(sizeB); float *h_C = (float *)malloc(sizeC); for (int i = 0; i < M * K; i++) h_A[i] = (float)(rand() % 100) / 100.0f; for (int i = 0; i < K * N; i++) h_B[i] = (float)(rand() % 100) / 100.0f; float *d_A, *d_B, *d_C; cudaMalloc(&d_A, sizeA); cudaMalloc(&d_B, sizeB); cudaMalloc(&d_C, sizeC); cudaMemcpy(d_A, h_A, sizeA, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); cudaMemcpy(d_B, h_B, sizeB, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // V1: 基础分块 printf("--- V1: 基础分块 (tile=%d) ---\n", TILE_SIZE); { dim3 block(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE); dim3 grid((N + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE, (M + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE); matMulTiled<<<grid, block>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) matMulTiled<<<grid, block>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); float gflops = 2.0f * M * N * K / (ms / 10 * 1e6); printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms\n", ms / 10); printf(" 性能: %.2f GFLOPS\n\n", gflops); } // V2: 寄存器 tile printf("--- V2: 寄存器 tile (%dx%d per thread) ---\n", RM, RN); { dim3 block(BN, BM); // 16x16 threads, covers 32x32 elements dim3 grid((N + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE, (M + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE); matMulRegTile<<<grid, block>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) matMulRegTile<<<grid, block>>>(d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); float gflops = 2.0f * M * N * K / (ms / 10 * 1e6); printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms\n", ms / 10); printf(" 性能: %.2f GFLOPS\n\n" // ... (后续代码省略)

直方图

PG 10.14, BPG 10.2.3

PG 10.14, BPG 10.2.3

week3_patterns/day18_histogram.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 18: 直方图 — 原子操作与共享内存 * ======================================= * 学习目标: * 1. 理解并行直方图的核心挑战: 数据竞争 * 2. 掌握 atomicAdd 的使用和性能影响 * 3. 学习共享内存直方图 (block 级局部直方图 + 全局归并) * 4. 理解 privatization 优化模式 * * 核心问题: * 多个线程可能同时更新同一个 bin → 数据竞争 * 解决方案 1: 全局原子操作 (简单但慢) * 解决方案 2: 共享内存局部直方图 + 原子归并 (快) * * 编译: nvcc day18_histogram.cu -o day18 * 运行: ./day18 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // V1: 全局内存原子直方图 (朴素) // 每个 thread 直接用 atomicAdd 更新全局直方图 // 问题: 全局原子操作非常慢 (可能 100+ cycle) // ============================================================ __global__ void histogramGlobal(const unsigned char *input, int *histogram, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { atomicAdd(&histogram[input[idx]], 1); } }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // V2: 共享内存直方图 (privatization 优化) * 每个 block 维护一个私有直方图在共享内存中 * 共享内存原子操作比全局快得多 * 最后将所有 block 的私有直方图归并到全局 // ============================================================ __global__ void histogramShared(const unsigned char *input, int *histogram, int n) { // 私有直方图在共享内存 __shared__ int s_hist[NUM_BINS]; // 初始化共享内存直方图 int tid = threadIdx.x; for (int i = tid; i < NUM_BINS; i += blockDim.x) { s_hist[i] = 0; } __syncthreads(); // 计算数据范围 int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int stride = blockDim.x * gridDim.x; // 每个 thread 处理多个元素 (grid-stride loop) for (int i = idx; i < n; i += stride) { // 共享内存原子操作 (快!) atomicAdd(&s_hist[input[i]], 1); } __syncthreads(); // 将局部直方图归并到全局 for (int i = tid; i < NUM_BINS; i += blockDim.x) { if (s_hist[i] > 0) { atomicAdd(&histogram[i], s_hist[i]); } } }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // V3: 适合少量 bin 的无冲突版本 // 当 bin 数 <= warpSize 时, 可以用 warp 级归约 // 这里展示另一种思路: 减少原子操作的竞争 // ============================================================ __global__ void histogramSharedOpt(const unsigned char *input, int *histogram, int n) { __shared__ int s_hist[NUM_BINS]; int tid = threadIdx.x; // 用线程索引初始化 (每线程初始化一个或多个 bin) for (int i = tid; i < NUM_BINS; i += blockDim.x) { s_hist[i] = 0; } __syncthreads(); // 每个 thread 处理一段连续数据 int elementsPerThread = (n + blockDim.x * gridDim.x - 1) / (blockDim.x * gridDim.x); int start = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) * elementsPerThread; int end = min(start + elementsPerThread, n); for (int i = start; i < end; i++) { atomicAdd(&s_hist[input[i]], 1); } __syncthreads(); // 归并 for (int i = tid; i < NUM_BINS; i += blockDim.x) { atomicAdd(&histogram[i], s_hist[i]); } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 18: 并行直方图\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 24; // 16M bytes printf("数据量: %d 字节 (%.2f MB)\n", n, (float)n / 1e6); printf("直方图 bin 数: %d\n\n", NUM_BINS); // 生成随机字节数据 unsigned char *h_data = (unsigned char *)malloc(n); srand(42); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_data[i] = (unsigned char)(rand() % NUM_BINS); } // CPU 参考 int h_ref[NUM_BINS] = {0}; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_ref[h_data[i]]++; } printf("CPU 直方图: bin[0]=%d, bin[128]=%d, bin[255]=%d\n\n", h_ref[0], h_ref[128], h_ref[255]); unsigned char *d_data; int *d_hist; cudaMalloc(&d_data, n); cudaMalloc(&d_hist, NUM_BINS * sizeof(int)); cudaMemcpy(d_data, h_data, n, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); int gridSize = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // V1: 全局原子 printf("--- V1: 全局原子直方图 ---\n"); { cudaMemset(d_hist, 0, NUM_BINS * sizeof(int)); histogramGlobal<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_data, d_hist, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); int h_hist[NUM_BINS]; cudaMemcpy(h_hist, d_hist, NUM_BINS * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) { cudaMemset(d_hist, 0, NUM_BINS * sizeof(int)); histogramGlobal<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_data, d_hist, n); } cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); bool ok = true; for (int i = 0; i < NUM_BINS; i++) { if (h_hist[i] != h_ref[i]) { ok = false; break; } } printf(" 耗时: %.3f ms (10次平均)\n", ms / 10); printf(" 吞吐: %.2f GB/s\n", n / (ms / 10 * 1e6)); printf(" 验证: %s\n\n", ok ? "✓ 通过" : "✗ 失败"); } // V2: 共享内存 printf("--- V2: 共享内存直方图 ---\n"); { cudaMemset(d_hist, 0, NUM_BINS * sizeof(int)); histogramShared<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_data, d_hist, n); cudaDeviceSynchronize(); int h_hist[NUM_BINS]; cudaMemcpy(h_hist, d_hist, NUM_BINS * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); cudaEventRecord(start); for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) { cudaMemset(d_hist, 0, NUM_BINS * sizeof(int)); // ... (后续代码省略)

流压缩

PG 10.22

PG 10.22
▶
week3_patterns/day19_compact.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * Day 19: 流压缩 (Compact) — Scan 的实战应用 * ============================================== * 学习目标: * 1. 理解流压缩: 从数组中过滤满足条件的元素 * 2. 使用 scan 计算每个元素的输出位置 * 3. 实现 "predicate → scan → scatter" 三步流水线 * 4. 理解为什么需要 exclusive scan * * 流压缩示例: * 输入: [3, 0, 5, 0, 2, 0, 7, 0] * 条件: 非零 * 掩码: [1, 0, 1, 0, 1, 0, 1, 0] * scan: [0, 1, 1, 2, 2, 3, 3, 4] (exclusive) * 输出: [3, 5, 2, 7] (长度=4) * * 应用: 过滤、去重、稀疏转密集 * * 编译: nvcc day19_compact.cu -o day19 * 运行: ./day19 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ============================================================ // Step 1: 生成 predicate 数组 (1=保留, 0=丢弃) // 这里以 "偶数" 为条件 // ============================================================ __global__ void generatePredicate(const int *input, int *predicate, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { predicate[idx] = (input[idx] % 2 == 0) ? 1 : 0; } }
Kernel 2
// ============================================================ // Step 2: Exclusive scan on predicate (计算输出位置) // 简化版: 每 block 内做 scan (Day16 的 Blelloch scan 简化版) // ============================================================ __global__ void scanPredicate(const int *predicate, int *scanned, int *blockSums, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; sdata[tid] = (idx < n) ? (float)predicate[idx] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); // Up-sweep int stride = 1; while (stride < BLOCK_SIZE) { int index = (tid + 1) * stride * 2 - 1; if (index < BLOCK_SIZE) { sdata[index] += sdata[index - stride]; } stride *= 2; __syncthreads(); } // 保存 block sum, 清零 if (tid == 0) { blockSums[blockIdx.x] = (int)sdata[BLOCK_SIZE - 1]; sdata[BLOCK_SIZE - 1] = 0.0f; } __syncthreads(); // Down-sweep stride = BLOCK_SIZE / 2; while (stride > 0) { int index = (tid + 1) * stride * 2 - 1; if (index < BLOCK_SIZE) { float tmp = sdata[index - stride]; sdata[index - stride] = sdata[index]; sdata[index] += tmp; } stride >>= 1; __syncthreads(); } if (idx < n) { scanned[idx] = (int)sdata[tid]; } }
Kernel 3
// ============================================================ // Step 2.5: 给每个 block 的 scan 结果加上前缀偏移 // ============================================================ __global__ void addOffsets(int *scanned, const int *blockSums, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n && blockIdx.x > 0) { int offset = 0; for (int i = 0; i < blockIdx.x; i++) offset += blockSums[i]; scanned[idx] += offset; } }
Kernel 4
// ============================================================ // Step 3: Scatter — 将满足条件的元素写入紧凑输出 // 只有 predicate=1 的元素才写入, 位置由 scanned 决定 // ============================================================ __global__ void scatterCompact(const int *input, const int *predicate, const int *scanned, int *output, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n && predicate[idx] == 1) { output[scanned[idx]] = input[idx]; } }
Kernel 5
// 计算总输出长度 (最后 block 的 scan 最大值 + 该 block 的 predicate 和) __global__ void computeOutputLen(const int *blockSums, int numBlocks, int *outLen) { if (threadIdx.x == 0) { int total = 0; for (int i = 0; i < numBlocks; i++) total += blockSums[i]; *outLen = total; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { printf("============================================\n"); printf(" Day 19: 流压缩 (Compact)\n"); printf("============================================\n\n"); int n = 1 << 20; // 1M size_t size = n * sizeof(int); printf("数据量: %d 元素\n", n); int *h_input = (int *)malloc(size); int *h_output = (int *)malloc(size); srand(42); int expectedCount = 0; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_input[i] = rand() % 1000; if (h_input[i] % 2 == 0) expectedCount++; } printf("条件: 偶数\n"); printf("期望输出长度: %d\n\n", expectedCount); int *d_input, *d_predicate, *d_scanned, *d_output, *d_blockSums, *d_outLen; cudaMalloc(&d_input, size); cudaMalloc(&d_predicate, size); cudaMalloc(&d_scanned, size); cudaMalloc(&d_output, size); cudaMemcpy(d_input, h_input, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice); int gridSize = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; cudaMalloc(&d_blockSums, gridSize * sizeof(int)); cudaMalloc(&d_outLen, sizeof(int)); cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); // 执行三步流水线 cudaEventRecord(start); // Step 1: predicate generatePredicate<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_predicate, n); // Step 2: scan scanPredicate<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_predicate, d_scanned, d_blockSums, n); addOffsets<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_scanned, d_blockSums, n); // Step 3: scatter scatterCompact<<<gridSize, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d_input, d_predicate, d_scanned, d_output, n); // 计算输出长度 computeOutputLen<<<1, 1>>>(d_blockSums, gridSize, d_outLen); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float ms; cudaEventElapsedTime(&ms, start, stop); // 获取结果 int outputLen; cudaMemcpy(&outputLen, d_outLen, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); cudaMemcpy(h_output, d_output, outputLen * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost); printf("--- 执行结果 ---\n"); printf(" 总耗时: %.3f ms\n", ms); printf(" 输出长度: %d (期望 %d)\n", outputLen, expectedCount); printf(" 验证: %s\n", outputLen == expectedCount ? "✓ 是" : "✗ 否"); // 打印示例 printf("\n 输入示例: "); for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) printf("%d ", h_input[i]); printf("...\n 输出示例: "); for (int i = 0; i < // ... (后续代码省略)

双调排序

BPG 12

BPG 12
