2D 卷积终极优化

BPG 10, 12

BPG 10, 12
▶
week9_mastery/day53_convolution_2d.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * day53_convolution_2d.cu — 2D 卷积终极优化 * * 学习目标: * - 综合运用常量内存、共享内存、异步拷贝等优化手段 * - 理解 halo region (光环区域) 的概念与实现 * - 掌握 2D 卷积从朴素到极致优化的完整路径 * * 核心概念: * - 常量内存: 卷积核体积小、所有线程只读 → 适合常量内存 + 广播缓存 * - 共享内存 tiling: 将图像分块加载到共享内存，减少全局内存访问 * - Halo region: 每个 tile 需要邻居像素 (卷积核半径范围)，称为光环 * - 异步拷贝 (cp.async): Ada架构支持，减少寄存器中转开销 * * 对应官方指南: * - BPG Ch10 (共享内存与全局内存) * - BPG Ch12 (优化策略综合) * - PG Ch6.2 (共享内存 API) * * 编译运行: * nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 day53_convolution_2d.cu -o day53 * ./day53 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ========================================================================= // V0: 朴素全局内存版本 // 每个线程对每个输出像素，直接从全局内存读取卷积核覆盖区域 // 缺点: 大量重复的全局内存访问 // ========================================================================= __global__ void conv2d_naive(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int width, int height, int radius) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 输出像素 x 坐标 int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; // 输出像素 y 坐标 if (x >= width || y >= height) return; float sum = 0.0f; for (int ky = -radius; ky <= radius; ky++) { for (int kx = -radius; kx <= radius; kx++) { // clamp 边界处理: 超出范围取边缘值 int ix = min(max(x + kx, 0), width - 1); int iy = min(max(y + ky, 0), height - 1); sum += input[iy * width + ix] * c_kernel[(ky + radius) * (2 * radius + 1) + (kx + radius)]; } } output[y * width + x] = sum; }
Kernel 2
// ========================================================================= // V0 已经使用了常量内存，V1 标记为"仅常量内存优化确认" // 后续 V2/V3 才是架构级优化 // ========================================================================= // V2: 共享内存 tile + halo region // 每个 block 加载一个 TILE_SIZE x TILE_SIZE 的 tile 及其 halo 到共享内存 // 线程协作加载: 内部线程 + halo 线程共同填充共享内存 // ========================================================================= __global__ void conv2d_shared(const float* __restrict__ input,
Kernel 3
__global__ void conv2d_async(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int width, int height, int radius) { __shared__ float s_tile[TILE_SIZE + 2 * MAX_KERNEL_RADIUS] [TILE_SIZE + 2 * MAX_KERNEL_RADIUS]; const int tile_dim = TILE_SIZE + 2 * radius; int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; int sx = threadIdx.x + radius; int sy = threadIdx.y + radius; int gx = min(max(x, 0), width - 1); int gy = min(max(y, 0), height - 1); // 使用异步拷贝加载主像素 __pipeline_memcpy_async(&s_tile[sy][sx], &input[gy * width + gx], sizeof(float)); // halo 加载 (同步方式，因为地址模式复杂) if (threadIdx.x < radius) { int hx = min(max(x - radius, 0), width - 1); s_tile[sy][threadIdx.x] = input[gy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.x >= TILE_SIZE - radius) { int hx = min(max(x + radius, 0), width - 1); s_tile[sy][sx + radius] = input[gy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.y < radius) { int hy = min(max(y - radius, 0), height - 1); s_tile[threadIdx.y][sx] = input[hy * width + gx]; if (threadIdx.x < radius) { s_tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = input[hy * width + min(max(x - radius, 0), width - 1)]; } if (threadIdx.x >= TILE_SIZE - radius) { s_tile[threadIdx.y][sx + radius] = input[hy * width + min(max(x + radius, 0), width - 1)]; } } if (threadIdx.y >= TILE_SIZE - radius) { int hy = min(max(y + radius, 0), height - 1); s_tile[sy + radius][sx] = input[hy * width + gx]; if (threadIdx.x < radius) { s_tile[sy + radius][threadIdx.x] = input[hy * width + min(max(x - radius, 0), width - 1)]; } if (threadIdx.x >= TILE_SIZE - radius) { s_tile[sy + radius][sx + radius] = input[hy * width + min(max(x + radius, 0), width - 1)]; } } // 等待异步拷贝完成 __pipeline_commit(); __pipeline_wait_prior(0); __syncthreads(); if (x < width && y < height) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int ky = -radius; ky <= radius; ky++) { for (int kx = -radius; kx <= radius; kx++) { sum += s_tile[sy + ky][sx + kx] * c_kernel[(ky + radius) * (2 * radius + 1) + (kx + radius)]; } } output[y * width + x] = sum; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { const int width = IMG_WIDTH; const int height = IMG_HEIGHT; const size_t img_size = (size_t)width * height * sizeof(float); printf("=== 2D 卷积终极优化 ===\n"); printf("图像尺寸: %d x %d (%.2f MB)\n\n", width, height, (double)img_size / (1024 * 1024)); // --- 分配并初始化主机数据 --- float* h_input = (float*)malloc(img_size); float* h_output = (float*)malloc(img_size); for (size_t i = 0; i < (size_t)width * height; i++) { h_input[i] = (float)(rand() % 256) / 255.0f; } // --- 分配设备内存 --- float *d_input, *d_output; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_input, img_size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_output, img_size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_input, h_input, img_size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); // --- 3x3 卷积核 (边缘检测) --- float kernel3x3[9] = { 0, -1, 0, -1, 5, -1, 0, -1, 0 }; // --- 5x5 高斯模糊核 --- float kernel5x5[25]; float sigma = 1.4f; float ksum = 0; for (int y = -2; y <= 2; y++) { for (int x = -2; x <= 2; x++) { float v = expf(-(float)(x*x + y*y) / (2.0f * sigma * sigma)); kernel5x5[(y+2)*5 + (x+2)] = v; ksum += v; } } for (int i = 0; i < 25; i++) kernel5x5[i] /= ksum; // --- 运行配置 --- dim3 block(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE); dim3 grid((width + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE, (height + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE); // ===================== 3x3 卷积测试 ===================== printf("--- 3x3 卷积核 (radius=1) ---\n"); set_kernel_to_constant(kernel3x3, 9); int radius3 = 1; // V0: 朴素版本 { void* args[] = {&d_input, &d_output, (void*)&width, (void*)&height, &radius3}; float ms = timing_kernel((const void*)conv2d_naive, grid, block, args); double gbytes = ((double)img_size * 2) / (ms / 1000.0) / 1e9; // 读+写 printf("V0 朴素全局内存: %8.3f ms 有效带宽: %6.1f GB/s\n", ms, gbytes); }
编译 & 运行
# 编译 nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 week9_mastery/day53_convolution_2d.cu -o week9_mastery/day53_convolution_2d # 运行 ./week9_mastery/day53_convolution_2d
54

N-Body 终极优化

PG 8, BPG 10-12

PG 8, BPG 10-12
▶
week9_mastery/day54_nbody_optimized.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * day54_nbody_optimized.cu — N-Body 终极优化 * * 学习目标: * - 掌握 O(N²) N体引力模拟的多种优化策略 * - 理解分块 (tiling) 对全局内存访问模式的影响 * - 学会循环展开与 L2 缓存局部性优化 * * 核心概念: * - N体问题: 计算每对质点间的万有引力，复杂度 O(N²) * - 分块: 将质点分批加载到共享内存，减少全局内存重复读取 * - 循环展开: 减少 warp 分支开销，提升 ILP * - L2 缓存局部性: 通过访问顺序优化提高缓存命中率 * * 对应官方指南: * - PG Ch8 (共享内存) * - BPG Ch10-12 (优化综合) * * 编译运行: * nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 day54_nbody_optimized.cu -o day54 * ./day54 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ========================================================================= // 质点数据结构: SoA (Structure of Arrays) 布局，利于合并访存 // ========================================================================= // ========================================================================= // V0: 朴素 O(N²) 版本 // 每个线程计算一个质点受力，直接遍历所有其他质点 // 全局内存访问: 每个线程读 N 个质点 → 总计 N² 次全局读取 // ========================================================================= __global__ void nbody_naive(const float4* __restrict__ bodies, // x,y,z = 位置, w = 质量 float4* __restrict__ forces, // x,y,z = 力, w = 0 int n, float dt) { int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (i >= n) return; float4 bi = bodies[i]; // 当前质点 float3 acc = {0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f}; // 遍历所有其他质点 for (int j = 0; j < n; j++) { float4 bj = bodies[j]; // 计算距离向量 float dx = bj.x - bi.x; float dy = bj.y - bi.y; float dz = bj.z - bi.z; // 距离平方 + 软化 float dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; float inv_dist = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); float inv_dist3 = inv_dist * inv_dist * inv_dist; // F = G * m1 * m2 / r² * (r_hat) float s = GRAVITY_G * bj.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; } // 更新力 (速度更新由 host 或另一个 kernel 处理) forces[i] = make_float4(acc.x * bi.w, acc.y * bi.w, acc.z * bi.w, 0.0f); }
Kernel 2
// ========================================================================= // V1: 共享内存分块 // 每个 block 加载一批质点 (p_tile) 到共享内存 // block 内所有线程从共享内存读取这批质点 → 共享内存广播 // 全局内存访问: N * (N / BLOCK_SIZE) = N²/BLOCK_SIZE 次全局读取 // 相比 V0 减少 BLOCK_SIZE 倍全局内存访问 // ========================================================================= __global__ void nbody_shared(const float4* __restrict__ bodies, float4* __restrict__ forces, int n, float dt) { int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; float4 bi = (i < n) ? bodies[i] : make_float4(0,0,0,0); float3 acc = {0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f}; __shared__ float4 p_tile[BLOCK_SIZE]; // 共享内存分块 // 遍历所有 tile int num_tiles = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; for (int t = 0; t < num_tiles; t++) { // 协作加载: 每个线程加载一个质点到共享内存 int idx = t * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x; p_tile[threadIdx.x] = (idx < n) ? bodies[idx] : make_float4(0,0,0,0); __syncthreads(); // 计算当前 tile 对质点 i 的贡献 for (int j = 0; j < BLOCK_SIZE; j++) { float4 bj = p_tile[j]; float dx = bj.x - bi.x; float dy = bj.y - bi.y; float dz = bj.z - bi.z; float dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; float inv_dist = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); float inv_dist3 = inv_dist * inv_dist * inv_dist; float s = GRAVITY_G * bj.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; } __syncthreads(); // 确保 tile 计算完成后再加载下一个 } if (i < n) { forces[i] = make_float4(acc.x * bi.w, acc.y * bi.w, acc.z * bi.w, 0.0f); } }
Kernel 3
// ========================================================================= // V2: V1 + 循环展开 // 将内层循环展开 UNROLL_FACTOR 次，减少循环开销 // 提升 ILP (指令级并行)，让编译器更好地调度 // ========================================================================= __global__ void nbody_unrolled(const float4* __restrict__ bodies, float4* __restrict__ forces, int n, float dt) { int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; float4 bi = (i < n) ? bodies[i] : make_float4(0,0,0,0); float3 acc = {0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f}; __shared__ float4 p_tile[BLOCK_SIZE]; int num_tiles = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; for (int t = 0; t < num_tiles; t++) { int idx = t * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x; p_tile[threadIdx.x] = (idx < n) ? bodies[idx] : make_float4(0,0,0,0); __syncthreads(); // 循环展开: 每次处理 UNROLL_FACTOR 个质点 int j = 0; for (; j + UNROLL_FACTOR <= BLOCK_SIZE; j += UNROLL_FACTOR) { // 展开 4 次 float4 bj0 = p_tile[j]; float4 bj1 = p_tile[j+1]; float4 bj2 = p_tile[j+2]; float4 bj3 = p_tile[j+3]; // 质点 0 float dx = bj0.x - bi.x; float dy = bj0.y - bi.y; float dz = bj0.z - bi.z; float dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; float inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; float s = GRAVITY_G * bj0.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; // 质点 1 dx = bj1.x - bi.x; dy = bj1.y - bi.y; dz = bj1.z - bi.z; dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; s = GRAVITY_G * bj1.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; // 质点 2 dx = bj2.x - bi.x; dy = bj2.y - bi.y; dz = bj2.z - bi.z; dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; s = GRAVITY_G * bj2.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; // 质点 3 dx = bj3.x - bi.x; dy = bj3.y - bi.y; dz = bj3.z - bi.z; dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; s = GRAVITY_G * bj3.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; } // 处理剩余 for (; j < BLOCK_SIZE; j++) { float4 bj = p_tile[j]; float dx = bj.x - bi.x; float dy = bj.y - bi.y; float dz = bj.z - bi.z; float dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; float inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; float s = GRAVITY_G * bj.w * inv_dist3; // ... (后续代码省略，请查看源文件)
Kernel 4
// ========================================================================= // V3: V2 + L2 缓存局部性优化 // 通过 __ldg() (只读缓存) 或 grid 维度排序优化 L2 命中率 // 使用 __ldg 读取 bodies，利用纹理缓存路径 // 同时调整 grid launch 顺序提升 L2 局部性 // ========================================================================= __global__ void nbody_l2(const float4* __restrict__ bodies, float4* __restrict__ forces, int n, float dt) { int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; // 使用 __ldg 通过只读缓存读取，提升 L2 命中率 float4 bi = (i < n) ? __ldg(&bodies[i]) : make_float4(0,0,0,0); float3 acc = {0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f}; __shared__ float4 p_tile[BLOCK_SIZE]; int num_tiles = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; for (int t = 0; t < num_tiles; t++) { int idx = t * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x; // 使用 __ldg 加载到共享内存 p_tile[threadIdx.x] = (idx < n) ? __ldg(&bodies[idx]) : make_float4(0,0,0,0); __syncthreads(); int j = 0; for (; j + UNROLL_FACTOR <= BLOCK_SIZE; j += UNROLL_FACTOR) { float4 bj0 = p_tile[j]; float4 bj1 = p_tile[j+1]; float4 bj2 = p_tile[j+2]; float4 bj3 = p_tile[j+3]; float dx = bj0.x - bi.x; float dy = bj0.y - bi.y; float dz = bj0.z - bi.z; float dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; float inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; float s = GRAVITY_G * bj0.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; dx = bj1.x - bi.x; dy = bj1.y - bi.y; dz = bj1.z - bi.z; dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; s = GRAVITY_G * bj1.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; dx = bj2.x - bi.x; dy = bj2.y - bi.y; dz = bj2.z - bi.z; dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; s = GRAVITY_G * bj2.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; dx = bj3.x - bi.x; dy = bj3.y - bi.y; dz = bj3.z - bi.z; dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; s = GRAVITY_G * bj3.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; } for (; j < BLOCK_SIZE; j++) { float4 bj = p_tile[j]; float dx = bj.x - bi.x; float dy = bj.y - bi.y; float dz = bj.z - bi.z; float dist_sqr = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz + EPS2; float inv_dist3 = rsqrtf(dist_sqr); inv_dist3 = inv_dist3 * inv_dist3 * inv_dist3; float s = GRAVITY_G * bj.w * inv_dist3; acc.x += dx * s; acc.y += dy * s; acc.z += dz * s; } __syncthreads(); // ... (后续代码省略，请查看源文件)
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { // 质点数量 const int N = 8192; const float dt = 0.01f; const size_t size = N * sizeof(float4); printf("=== N-Body 终极优化 ===\n"); printf("质点数: %d\n\n", N); // --- 初始化质点数据 --- float4* h_bodies = (float4*)malloc(size); srand(42); for (int i = 0; i < N; i++) { h_bodies[i].x = (float)(rand() % 1000) / 100.0f; h_bodies[i].y = (float)(rand() % 1000) / 100.0f; h_bodies[i].z = (float)(rand() % 1000) / 100.0f; h_bodies[i].w = 1.0f; // 质量 = 1 } // --- 分配设备内存 --- float4 *d_bodies, *d_forces; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_bodies, size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_forces, size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_bodies, h_bodies, size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); // --- 运行各版本 --- printf("--- 性能对比 ---\n\n"); // V0: 朴素 float t0 = run_and_time(nbody_naive, d_bodies, d_forces, N, dt); // 每次计算: N 次距离计算，每次约 20 FLOPS → 总计 ~20*N*N FLOPS double gflops0 = (20.0 * N * N) / (t0 / 1000.0) / 1e9; printf("V0 朴素 O(N²): %8.3f ms GFLOPS: %7.1f\n", t0, gflops0); // V1: 共享内存分块 float t1 = run_and_time(nbody_shared, d_bodies, d_forces, N, dt); double gflops1 = (20.0 * N * N) / (t1 / 1000.0) / 1e9; printf("V1 共享内存分块: %8.3f ms GFLOPS: %7.1f\n", t1, gflops1); // V2: V1 + 循环展开 float t2 = run_and_time(nbody_unrolled, d_bodies, d_forces, N, dt); double gflops2 = (20.0 * N * N) / (t2 / 1000.0) / 1e9; printf("V2 共享内存+展开: %8.3f ms GFLOPS: %7.1f\n", t2, gflops2); // V3: V2 + L2 缓存优化 float t3 = run_and_time(nbody_l2, d_bodies, d_forces, N, dt); double gflops3 = (20.0 * N * N) / (t3 / 1000.0) / 1e9; printf("V3 共享内存+展开+L2: %8.3f ms GFLOPS: %7.1f\n", t3, gflops3); // --- 性能对比表格 --- printf("\n=== 性能对比表格 ===\n"); printf("+------------------------+----------+----------+------------+\n"); printf("| 版本 | 时间(ms) | GFLOPS | 加速比 |\n"); printf("+------------------------+----------+----------+------------+\n"); printf("| V0 朴素 O(N²) | %8.3f | %8.1f | 1.00x |\n", t0, gflops0); printf("| V1 共享内存分块 | %8.3f | %8.1f | %.2fx |\n", t1, gflops1, t0/t1); printf("| V2 共享内存+循环展开 | %8.3f | %8.1f | %.2fx |\n", t2, gflops2, t0/t2); printf("| V3 共享内存+展开+L2 | %8.3f | %8.1f | %.2fx |\n", t3, gflops3, t0/t3); printf("+------------------------+----------+----------+------------+\n"); printf("| 优化要点: // ... (后续代码省略)

Tensor Core 矩阵乘法

PG 10.24

PG 10.24
▶
week9_mastery/day55_tensor_matmul.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * day55_tensor_matmul.cu — Tensor Core 矩阵乘法 * * 学习目标: * - 理解 Tensor Core 与 WMMA (Warp Matrix Functions) API * - 掌握 FP16 半精度矩阵乘法的实现 * - 对比 FP32 朴素/共享内存/WMMA 各版本的性能差异 * * 核心概念: * - Tensor Core: 专用矩阵计算单元，一个时钟周期完成 D = A*B + C * - WMMA API: warp 级矩阵操作，一个 warp 协作处理 16x16x16 矩阵块 * - FP16 半精度: 2倍数据密度，Tensor Core 专用精度 * - fragment: WMMA 的矩阵片段，存储在寄存器中 * * 对应官方指南: * - PG Ch10.24 (Warp Matrix Functions) * - BPG Ch12 (优化策略) * * 编译运行: * nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 day55_tensor_matmul.cu -o day55 * ./day55 * * 注意: WMMA 需要 sm_70+，本代码针对 sm_89 (Ada Lovelace) */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ========================================================================= // V0: FP32 朴素矩阵乘法 (基线) // C = A * B, A: MxK, B: KxN, C: MxN // 每个线程计算 C 的一个元素，朴素三重循环 // ========================================================================= __global__ void matmul_naive_fp32(const float* __restrict__ A, const float* __restrict__ B, float* __restrict__ C, int M, int N, int K) { int row = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; int col = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (row >= M || col >= N) return; float sum = 0.0f; for (int i = 0; i < K; i++) { sum += A[row * K + i] * B[i * N + col]; } C[row * N + col] = sum; }
Kernel 2
__global__ void matmul_shared_fp32(const float* __restrict__ A, const float* __restrict__ B, float* __restrict__ C, int M, int N, int K) { __shared__ float s_A[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE]; __shared__ float s_B[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE]; int row = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; int col = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; float sum = 0.0f; // 遍历 K 维度的 tile for (int t = 0; t < (K + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE; t++) { // 协作加载 A 的 tile int a_row = row; int a_col = t * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; s_A[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = (a_row < M && a_col < K) ? A[a_row * K + a_col] : 0.0f; // 协作加载 B 的 tile int b_row = t * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; int b_col = col; s_B[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = (b_row < K && b_col < N) ? B[b_row * N + b_col] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); // 计算部分和 for (int i = 0; i < TILE_SIZE; i++) { sum += s_A[threadIdx.y][i] * s_B[i][threadIdx.x]; } __syncthreads(); } if (row < M && col < N) { C[row * N + col] = sum; } }
Kernel 3
// ========================================================================= // V2: WMMA FP16 半精度矩阵乘法 // 使用 wmma::fragment 进行 16x16x16 矩阵乘法 // 一个 warp 处理一个 16x16 的输出 tile // A: FP16 (MxK), B: FP16 (KxN), C: FP32 (MxN) [accumulate in FP32] // ========================================================================= __global__ void matmul_wmma_fp16(const __half* __restrict__ A, const __half* __restrict__ B, float* __restrict__ C, int M, int N, int K) { // WMMA fragment 声明 wmma::fragment<wmma::matrix_a, WMMA_M, WMMA_N, WMMA_K, __half, wmma::row_major> a_frag; wmma::fragment<wmma::matrix_b, WMMA_M, WMMA_N, WMMA_K, __half, wmma::row_major> b_frag; wmma::fragment<wmma::accumulator, WMMA_M, WMMA_N, WMMA_K, float> c_frag; // 初始化累加器为 0 wmma::fill_fragment(c_frag, 0.0f); // warp 索引: 每个 warp 处理一个 16x16 的输出 tile int warp_id = (threadIdx.y * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / WARP_SIZE; int warps_per_block = (blockDim.x * blockDim.y) / WARP_SIZE; // 输出 tile 坐标 int tile_row = (blockIdx.y * warps_per_block + warp_id) * WMMA_M; int tile_col = blockIdx.x * WMMA_N; // 遍历 K 维度 for (int k = 0; k < K; k += WMMA_K) { // 加载 A 和 B 的 fragment wmma::load_matrix_sync(a_frag, A + tile_row * K + k, K); wmma::load_matrix_sync(b_frag, B + k * N + tile_col, N); // 矩阵乘加: c_frag += a_frag * b_frag wmma::mma_sync(c_frag, a_frag, b_frag, c_frag); } // 存储结果到 C wmma::store_matrix_sync(C + tile_row * N + tile_col, c_frag, N, wmma::mem_row_major); }
Kernel 4
// K 方向每次处理 WMMA_K=16 __global__ void matmul_wmma_shared(const __half* __restrict__ A, const __half* __restrict__ B, float* __restrict__ C, int M, int N, int K) { // 共享内存: 存放 A 和 B 的 tile __shared__ __half s_A[BLOCK_M][WMMA_K]; // BLOCK_M x 16 __shared__ __half s_B[WMMA_K][BLOCK_N]; // 16 x BLOCK_N // 每个 block 有 4 个 warp (2x2 排列)，每个处理 16x16 输出 int warp_id = threadIdx.x / WARP_SIZE; int warp_row = warp_id / 2; // 0 or 1 int warp_col = warp_id % 2; // 0 or 1 int block_row = blockIdx.y * BLOCK_M; int block_col = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_N; // fragment wmma::fragment<wmma::matrix_a, WMMA_M, WMMA_N, WMMA_K, __half, wmma::row_major> a_frag; wmma::fragment<wmma::matrix_b, WMMA_M, WMMA_N, WMMA_K, __half, wmma::row_major> b_frag; wmma::fragment<wmma::accumulator, WMMA_M, WMMA_N, WMMA_K, float> c_frag; wmma::fill_fragment(c_frag, 0.0f); // 遍历 K 维度，每次 WMMA_K=16 for (int k = 0; k < K; k += WMMA_K) { // 协作加载 A 和 B 到共享内存 // 128 线程加载 BLOCK_M(64) x 16 = 1024 个 __half (A tile) // 每个 warp 加载一行 (32 个元素) int load_row = warp_id * 2 + (threadIdx.x % 2); // 简化加载模式 int load_col = threadIdx.x / 2; if (load_row < BLOCK_M && load_col < WMMA_K) { int global_row = block_row + load_row; s_A[load_row][load_col] = (global_row < M) ? A[global_row * K + k + load_col] : __float2half(0.0f); } // 加载 B tile: 16 x BLOCK_N load_row = threadIdx.x / (BLOCK_N / 2); // 0~15 load_col = (threadIdx.x % (BLOCK_N / 2)) * 2; if (load_row < WMMA_K && load_col < BLOCK_N) { int global_col = block_col + load_col; s_B[load_row][load_col] = (k + load_row < K && global_col < N) ? B[(k + load_row) * N + global_col] : __float2half(0.0f); if (load_col + 1 < BLOCK_N) { s_B[load_row][load_col + 1] = (k + load_row < K && global_col + 1 < N) ? B[(k + load_row) * N + global_col + 1] : __float2half(0.0f); } } __syncthreads(); // 从共享内存加载 fragment int a_row = warp_row * WMMA_M; int b_col = warp_col * WMMA_N; wmma::load_matrix_sync(a_frag, &s_A[a_row][0], WMMA_K); wmma::load_matrix_sync(b_frag, &s_B[0][b_col], BLOCK_N); wmma::mma_sync(c_frag, a_frag, b_frag, c_frag); __syncthreads(); } // 存储 int out_row = block_row + warp_row * WMMA_M; int out_col = block_col + warp_col * WMMA_N; wmma::store_matrix_sync(C + out_row * N + out_col, c_frag, N, wmma::mem_row_major); }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { const int M = MATRIX_M, N = MATRIX_N, K = MATRIX_K; const size_t size_fp32 = (size_t)M * K * sizeof(float); const size_t size_fp16 = (size_t)M * K * sizeof(__half); printf("=== Tensor Core 矩阵乘法 ===\n"); printf("矩阵尺寸: %dx%d x %dx%d\n\n", M, K, K, N); // --- 分配主机内存 --- float* h_A = (float*)malloc(size_fp32); float* h_B = (float*)malloc(size_fp32); float* h_C = (float*)malloc(M * N * sizeof(float)); // 初始化 (小随机值，避免 FP16 溢出) srand(42); for (int i = 0; i < M * K; i++) h_A[i] = (float)(rand() % 100) / 1000.0f; for (int i = 0; i < K * N; i++) h_B[i] = (float)(rand() % 100) / 1000.0f; // --- FP16 版本的主机数据 --- __half* h_A_fp16 = (__half*)malloc(size_fp16); __half* h_B_fp16 = (__half*)malloc(size_fp16); for (int i = 0; i < M * K; i++) h_A_fp16[i] = __float2half(h_A[i]); for (int i = 0; i < K * N; i++) h_B_fp16[i] = __float2half(h_B[i]); // --- 分配设备内存 --- float *d_A, *d_B, *d_C; __half *d_A_fp16, *d_B_fp16; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_A, size_fp32)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_B, size_fp32)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_C, M * N * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_A_fp16, size_fp16)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_B_fp16, size_fp16)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_A, h_A, size_fp32, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_B, h_B, size_fp32, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_A_fp16, h_A_fp16, size_fp16, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_B_fp16, h_B_fp16, size_fp16, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); // --- 运行各版本 --- // V0: 朴素 FP32 dim3 block0(32, 32); dim3 grid0((N + 31) / 32, (M + 31) / 32); float t0 = run_and_time(matmul_naive_fp32, grid0, block0, d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); double gflops0 = 2.0 * M * N * K / (t0 / 1000.0) / 1e9; printf("V0 朴素 FP32: %8.3f ms GFLOPS: %7.1f\n", t0, gflops0); // V1: 共享内存 FP32 dim3 block1(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE); dim3 grid1((N + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE, (M + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE); float t1 = run_and_time(matmul_shared_fp32, grid1, block1, d_A, d_B, d_C, M, N, K); double gflops1 = 2.0 * M * N * K / (t1 / 1000.0) / 1e9; printf("V1 共享内存 FP32: %8.3f ms GFLOPS: %7.1f\n", t1, gflops1); // V2: WMMA FP16 (每 warp 16x16 tile, grid 覆盖整个矩阵) // 每个 block: 4 warp = 128 线程, 处理 64x16 输出 (2 行 warp x 1 列 warp) // 简化: 1 warp per block, 16x16 output // ... week9_mastery/day56_graph_pipeline.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * day56_graph_pipeline.cu — Graph + 流水线优化 * * 学习目标: * - 掌握 CUDA Graph 的创建、实例化和重复执行 * - 理解多 kernel 管线的优化策略 * - 学会使用多流并行执行多个管线实例 * * 核心概念: * - CUDA Graph: 将多个 kernel/操作打包为图，减少 CPU 启动开销 * - 管线 (Pipeline): 数据拷贝→预处理→计算→后处理→拷贝回 * - 多流并行: 不同流中的管线可以重叠执行 * - 异步拷贝: cudaMemcpyAsync 与 kernel 计算重叠 * * 对应官方指南: * - PG Ch6.2.8.7 (CUDA Graphs) * - PG Ch10.28 (Graph 优化) * * 编译运行: * nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 day56_graph_pipeline.cu -o day56 * ./day56 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 1: 预处理 (归一化: x = (x - min) / (max - min)) // 简化版: 每个元素乘以缩放因子 // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_preprocess(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int n, float scale) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { output[idx] = input[idx] * scale; } }
Kernel 2
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 2: 计算 (平方运算) // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_compute(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int n) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { float v = input[idx]; output[idx] = v * v; } }
Kernel 3
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 3: 后处理 (加偏置) // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_postprocess(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int n, float bias) { int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (idx < n) { output[idx] = input[idx] + bias; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { const int n = DATA_SIZE; const size_t size = n * sizeof(float); printf("=== Graph + 流水线优化 ===\n"); printf("数据量: %d 元素 (%.2f MB), 迭代次数: %d\n\n", n, (double)size / (1024*1024), ITERATIONS); // --- 分配主机内存 (普通 + pinned) --- float* h_input = (float*)malloc(size); float* h_output = (float*)malloc(size); float *h_input_pinned, *h_output_pinned; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_input_pinned, size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&h_output_pinned, size)); // 初始化 srand(42); for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { h_input[i] = h_input_pinned[i] = (float)(rand() % 1000) / 1000.0f; } // --- 分配设备内存 --- float *d_in, *d_tmp1, *d_tmp2, *d_out; 高斯模糊完整实现

综合

综合

week9_mastery/day57_gaussian_blur.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * day57_gaussian_blur.cu — 高斯模糊完整实现 * * 学习目标: * - 理解可分离滤波器原理 (2D 卷积分解为两个 1D 卷积) * - 掌握共享内存 tiling 在图像处理中的应用 * - 学会多通道 (RGB) 处理与边界处理 (clamp) * * 核心概念: * - 高斯模糊: 用高斯函数作为卷积核对图像进行低通滤波 * - 可分离性: 2D 高斯核 = 1D 水平核 × 1D 垂直核，将 O(K²) 降为 O(2K) * - 共享内存 tiling: 将图像分块加载，减少全局内存访问 * - 常量内存: 1D 高斯核尺寸小、全线程只读 → 常量内存 * * 对应官方指南: * - BPG Ch10-12 综合 * - PG Ch6.2 (共享内存) * * 编译运行: * nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 day57_gaussian_blur.cu -o day57 * ./day57 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ========================================================================= // V0: 朴素 2D 卷积 (不可分离) // 直接用 2D 高斯核做卷积，每个像素 O(K²) 计算 // 每通道独立处理 // ========================================================================= __global__ void gaussian_blur_naive2d(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int width, int height, int radius, int channels) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; if (x >= width || y >= height) return; // 对每个通道做 2D 卷积 for (int c = 0; c < channels; c++) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int ky = -radius; ky <= radius; ky++) { for (int kx = -radius; kx <= radius; kx++) { int ix = min(max(x + kx, 0), width - 1); int iy = min(max(y + ky, 0), height - 1); // 2D 核 = 1D 水平 × 1D 垂直 float kv = c_gaussian_kernel[kx + radius] * c_gaussian_kernel[ky + radius]; sum += input[(iy * width + ix) * channels + c] * kv; } } output[(y * width + x) * channels + c] = sum; } }
Kernel 2
// ========================================================================= // V1: 可分离滤波器 (先水平后垂直，两遍 pass) // Pass 1: 水平模糊 → 中间结果 (存在临时缓冲区) // Pass 2: 垂直模糊 → 最终结果 // 计算量从 O(K²) 降为 O(2K)，减少 radius 倍 // ========================================================================= // Pass 1: 水平方向 1D 卷积 __global__ void gaussian_blur_horizontal(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ temp, int width, int height, int radius, int channels) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; if (x >= width || y >= height) return; for (int c = 0; c < channels; c++) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int k = -radius; k <= radius; k++) { int ix = min(max(x + k, 0), width - 1); sum += input[(y * width + ix) * channels + c] * c_gaussian_kernel[k + radius]; } temp[(y * width + x) * channels + c] = sum; } }
Kernel 3
// Pass 2: 垂直方向 1D 卷积 __global__ void gaussian_blur_vertical(const float* __restrict__ temp, float* __restrict__ output, int width, int height, int radius, int channels) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; if (x >= width || y >= height) return; for (int c = 0; c < channels; c++) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int k = -radius; k <= radius; k++) { int iy = min(max(y + k, 0), height - 1); sum += temp[(iy * width + x) * channels + c] * c_gaussian_kernel[k + radius]; } output[(y * width + x) * channels + c] = sum; } }
Kernel 4
// ========================================================================= // V2: V1 + 共享内存 tiling (水平 pass) // 将图像块加载到共享内存，减少全局内存读取 // 水平 pass: 每行加载 TILE_W + 2*radius 个元素 // ========================================================================= __global__ void gaussian_blur_h_shared(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ temp, int width, int height, int radius, int channels) { // 共享内存: 每行 TILE_W + 2*radius 个 float __shared__ float s_data[TILE_H][TILE_W + 2 * MAX_RADIUS]; int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_W + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_H + threadIdx.y; int tx = threadIdx.x + radius; // 共享内存中的偏移 // 当前线程处理的通道 for (int c = 0; c < channels; c++) { // 加载主区域 int gx = min(max(x, 0), width - 1); int gy = min(max(y, 0), height - 1); if (threadIdx.y < TILE_H && y < height) { s_data[threadIdx.y][tx] = input[(gy * width + gx) * channels + c]; } // 加载左 halo if (threadIdx.x < radius) { int hx = min(max(x - radius, 0), width - 1); s_data[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = input[(gy * width + hx) * channels + c]; } // 加载右 halo if (threadIdx.x >= TILE_W - radius) { int hx = min(max(x + radius, 0), width - 1); s_data[threadIdx.y][tx + radius] = input[(gy * width + hx) * channels + c]; } __syncthreads(); // 水平卷积 if (x < width && y < height) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int k = -radius; k <= radius; k++) { sum += s_data[threadIdx.y][tx + k] * c_gaussian_kernel[k + radius]; } temp[(y * width + x) * channels + c] = sum; } __syncthreads(); } }
Kernel 5
// 垂直 pass 的共享内存版本 __global__ void gaussian_blur_v_shared(const float* __restrict__ temp, float* __restrict__ output, int width, int height, int radius, int channels) { __shared__ float s_data[TILE_H + 2 * MAX_RADIUS][TILE_W]; int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_W + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_H + threadIdx.y; int ty = threadIdx.y + radius; for (int c = 0; c < channels; c++) { int gx = min(max(x, 0), width - 1); int gy = min(max(y, 0), height - 1); if (x < width) { s_data[ty][threadIdx.x] = temp[(gy * width + gx) * channels + c]; } // 上 halo if (threadIdx.y < radius) { int hy = min(max(y - radius, 0), height - 1); s_data[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = temp[(hy * width + gx) * channels + c]; } // 下 halo if (threadIdx.y >= TILE_H - radius) { int hy = min(max(y + radius, 0), height - 1); s_data[ty + radius][threadIdx.x] = temp[(hy * width + gx) * channels + c]; } __syncthreads(); if (x < width && y < height) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int k = -radius; k <= radius; k++) { sum += s_data[ty + k][threadIdx.x] * c_gaussian_kernel[k + radius]; } output[(y * width + x) * channels + c] = sum; } __syncthreads(); } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { const int width = IMG_WIDTH, height = IMG_HEIGHT; const int channels = CHANNELS; const size_t img_size = (size_t)width * height * channels * sizeof(float); printf("=== 高斯模糊完整实现 ===\n"); printf("图像: %dx%d, %d 通道 (%.2f MB)\n\n", width, height, channels, (double)img_size / (1024*1024)); // --- 生成高斯核 --- int radius = 7; // 15x15 核 float sigma = 4.0f; float kernel[2 * MAX_RADIUS + 1]; generate_gaussian_kernel(kernel, radius, sigma); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(c_gaussian_kernel, kernel, (2 * radius + 1) * sizeof(float))); printf("高斯核: radius=%d, sigma=%.1f (%d taps)\n\n", radius, sigma, 2*radius+1); // --- 分配主机内存 --- float* h_input = (float*)malloc(img_size); float* h_output = (float*)malloc(img_size); srand(42); for (size_t i = 0; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_output, img_size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_input, h_input, img_size, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); // --- 运行配置 --- dim3 block(TILE_W, TILE_H); dim3 grid((width + TILE_W - 1) / TILE_W, (height + TILE_H - 1) / TILE_H); float total = ms1 + ms2; double gbytes = ((double)img_size * 3) / (total / 1000.0) / 1e9; // 读输入+写中间+写输出 printf("V1 可分离(水平%.3f+垂直%.3f): %8.3f ms 带宽: %6.1f GB/s\n", ms1, ms2, tota // ... 稀疏矩阵向量乘

week9_mastery/day58_sparse_vector.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * day58_sparse_vector.cu — 稀疏矩阵向量乘 (SpMV) * * 学习目标: * - 掌握 CSR 和 ELL 稀疏矩阵格式的 SpMV 实现 * - 理解 warp 级并行与共享内存缓存优化 * - 学会评估 SpMV 的带宽利用率 * * 核心概念: * - SpMV: y = A*x, A 为稀疏矩阵, x/y 为稠密向量 * - CSR (Compressed Sparse Row): values[], col_indices[], row_ptr[] * - ELL (ELLPACK): 固定每行非零元数，coalesced 访问 * - 带宽利用率: SpMV 通常是带宽受限的，关注有效带宽 * * 对应官方指南: * - BPG Ch10-12 综合 * * 编译运行: * nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 day58_sparse_vector.cu -o day58 * ./day58 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// row_ptr[]: 行指针数组, 长度 = num_rows + 1 // row_ptr[i] 到 row_ptr[i+1] 是第 i 行的非零元范围 // ========================================================================= // ========================================================================= // V0: 朴素 CSR SpMV (每行一个线程) // 每个线程处理一行，串行累加该行所有非零元 // 缺点: 行长度不均导致 warp divergence；长行串行计算 // ========================================================================= __global__ void spmv_csr_naive(const float* __restrict__ values, const int* __restrict__ col_indices, const int* __restrict__ row_ptr, const float* __restrict__ x, float* __restrict__ y, int num_rows) { int row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (row >= num_rows) return; float sum = 0.0f; int start = row_ptr[row]; int end = row_ptr[row + 1]; for (int i = start; i < end; i++) { sum += values[i] * x[col_indices[i]]; } y[row] = sum; }
Kernel 2
// ========================================================================= // V1: Warp 级 CSR SpMV (每 warp 处理一行) // 一个 warp (32线程) 协作处理一行 // 行内非零元由 warp 内线程并行处理，最后 warp 归约求和 // 优势: 长行并行化，减少串行累加 // ========================================================================= __global__ void spmv_csr_warp(const float* __restrict__ values, const int* __restrict__ col_indices, const int* __restrict__ row_ptr, const float* __restrict__ x, float* __restrict__ y, int num_rows) { int warp_id = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / WARP_SIZE; int lane = threadIdx.x % WARP_SIZE; if (warp_id >= num_rows) return; int row_start = row_ptr[warp_id]; int row_end = row_ptr[warp_id + 1]; int row_len = row_end - row_start; float sum = 0.0f; // warp 内线程协作遍历该行非零元 for (int i = row_start + lane; i < row_end; i += WARP_SIZE) { sum += values[i] * x[col_indices[i]]; } // warp 归约求和 for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) { sum += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffff, sum, offset); } // lane 0 写入结果 if (lane == 0) { y[warp_id] = sum; } }
Kernel 3
// ========================================================================= // V2: V1 + 共享内存缓存 x 向量 // 将 x 向量的部分数据缓存到共享内存 // 当 col_indices 重复访问 x 的相同区域时，减少全局内存访问 // 注意: 对于通用稀疏矩阵，x 访问模式不规则，缓存效果取决于矩阵结构 // 这里采用缓存 x 的一个分块策略 // ========================================================================= __global__ void spmv_csr_warp_shared(const float* __restrict__ values, const int* __restrict__ col_indices, const int* __restrict__ row_ptr, const float* __restrict__ x, float* __restrict__ y, int num_rows) { int warp_id = (blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x) / WARP_SIZE; int lane = threadIdx.x % WARP_SIZE; if (warp_id >= num_rows) return; int row_start = row_ptr[warp_id]; int row_end = row_ptr[warp_id + 1]; float sum = 0.0f; // warp 协作遍历 for (int i = row_start + lane; i < row_end; i += WARP_SIZE) { // 直接从全局内存读取 x (配合 L2 缓存) sum += values[i] * __ldg(&x[col_indices[i]]); } // warp 归约 for (int offset = WARP_SIZE / 2; offset > 0; offset /= 2) { sum += __shfl_xor_sync(0xffffffff, sum, offset); } if (lane == 0) { y[warp_id] = sum; } }
Kernel 4
// ========================================================================= // V3: ELL 格式 SpMV * ELL 格式: 每行固定 ELL_WIDTH 个非零元 (不足的补零) * data[ELL_WIDTH * num_rows]: 列优先存储非零值 * indices[ELL_WIDTH * num_rows]: 列优先存储列索引 * 优势: 规则内存访问，无分歧，coalesced * 劣势: 若行长度差异大，浪费存储和计算 // ========================================================================= __global__ void spmv_ell(const float* __restrict__ data, const int* __restrict__ indices, const float* __restrict__ x, float* __restrict__ y, int num_rows, int ell_width) { int row = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (row >= num_rows) return; float sum = 0.0f; // ELL 列优先存储: data[col * num_rows + row] for (int c = 0; c < ell_width; c++) { int idx = c * num_rows + row; int col = indices[idx]; if (col >= 0) { // -1 表示 padding sum += data[idx] * x[col]; } } y[row] = sum; }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { const int num_rows = 8192; const int num_cols = 8192; const float density = 0.01f; // 1% 非零元 printf("=== 稀疏矩阵向量乘 (SpMV) ===\n"); printf("矩阵: %dx%d, 密度: %.2f%%\n\n", num_rows, num_cols, density * 100); // --- 生成随机稀疏矩阵 (CSR) --- float* h_values; int* h_col_indices; int* h_row_ptr; int nnz; generate_random_sparse_csr(num_rows, num_cols, density, &h_values, &h_col_indices, &h_row_ptr, &nnz); printf("非零元数 (nnz): %d\n\n", nnz); // --- 生成 x 向量 --- float* h_x = (float*)malloc(num_cols * sizeof(float)); for (int i = 0; i < num_cols; i++) h_x[i] = (float)(rand() % 100) / 100.0f; // --- 转换为 ELL --- float* h_ell_data; int* h_ell_indices; int ell_width; csr_to_ell(h_values, h_col_indices, h_row_ptr, num_rows, &h_ell_data, &h_ell_indices, &ell_width); printf("ELL 宽度 (max row len): %d\n", ell_width); // --- 分配设备内存 (CSR) --- float *d_values, *d_x, *d_y; int *d_col_indices, *d_row_ptr; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_values, nnz * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_col_indices, nnz * sizeof(int))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_row_ptr, (num_rows + 1) * sizeof(int))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_x, num_cols * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_y, num_rows * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_values, h_values, nnz * sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_col_indices, h_col_indices, nnz * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_row_ptr, h_row_ptr, (num_rows + 1) * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_x, h_x, num_cols * sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); // --- 分配设备内存 (ELL) --- size_t ell_size = (size_t)ell_width * num_rows; float *d_ell_data; int *d_ell_indices; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_ell_data, ell_size * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_ell_indices, ell_size * sizeof(int))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_ell_data, h_ell_data, ell_size * sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_ell_indices, h_ell_indices, ell_size * sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice)); // --- 运行各版本 --- cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); int iterations = 100; printf("\n--- 性能对比 ---\n\n");

并行异常检测

综合

综合
▶
week9_mastery/day59_anomaly_detection.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * day59_anomaly_detection.cu — 并行异常检测 (APOD 全流程) * * 学习目标: * - 完整实践 APOD (Assess, Parallelize, Optimize, Deploy) 流程 * - 掌握多 kernel 协作管线: 归约 → map → scan → sort * - 理解流压缩 (stream compaction) 的实现 * * 核心概念: * - 异常检测: 找偏离均值超过 N 个标准差的数据点 * - 归约 (Reduction): 并行求和计算均值/方差 * - 流压缩 (Stream Compaction): 用 scan + scatter 提取满足条件的元素 * - Bitonic Sort: 并行排序网络，适合 GPU * * APOD 流程: * 1. Assess: CPU 基准实现 * 2. Parallelize: GPU 并行实现各步骤 * 3. Optimize: 共享内存归约、warp 原语优化 * 4. Deploy: 多 kernel 协作管线 * * 对应官方指南: * - 全部综合 (BPG Ch10-12, PG Ch6-10) * * 编译运行: * nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 day59_anomaly_detection.cu -o day59 * ./day59 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ========================================================================= // 步骤 1: 并行归约求和 (计算总和，用于求均值) // 使用共享内存 + 树形归约 // 每个 block 归约 BLOCK_SIZE 个元素，输出部分和 // ========================================================================= __global__ void reduce_sum(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ partial, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid; // 加载数据到共享内存 sdata[tid] = (i < n) ? input[i] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); // 树形归约 for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) { if (tid < s) { sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + s]; } __syncthreads(); } // block 的第一个线程写入部分和 if (tid == 0) { partial[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; } }
Kernel 2
// ========================================================================= // 步骤 2: 并行归约计算平方和 (用于方差) // 计算 sum((x - mean)²)，输入是 (x - mean)² 数组 // ========================================================================= __global__ void reduce_sumsq(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ partial, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid; sdata[tid] = (i < n) ? input[i] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) { if (tid < s) sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + s]; __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) partial[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; }
Kernel 3
// ========================================================================= // 辅助 kernel: 计算每个元素与均值的偏差平方 // input[i] = (data[i] - mean)² // ========================================================================= __global__ void compute_deviation_sq(const float* __restrict__ data, float* __restrict__ output, int n, float mean) { int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (i < n) { float diff = data[i] - mean; output[i] = diff * diff; } }
Kernel 4
// ========================================================================= // 步骤 3: 并行标记异常点 (map + predicate) // 如果 |x - mean| > threshold * stddev → 标记为 1，否则 0 // 输出 flags 数组 (0 或 1) // 同时计算 deviation (偏离程度 = |x - mean| / stddev) // ========================================================================= __global__ void mark_anomalies(const float* __restrict__ data, const float* __restrict__ deviation_sq, int* __restrict__ flags, float* __restrict__ deviations, int n, float mean, float stddev, float threshold) { int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (i < n) { float dev = sqrtf(deviation_sq[i]) / stddev; // 偏离程度 (单位: 标准差) deviations[i] = dev; flags[i] = (dev > threshold) ? 1 : 0; } }
Kernel 5
// ========================================================================= // 步骤 4a: 并行前缀扫描 (exclusive scan) // 对 flags 数组做前缀和，用于确定每个异常点的输出位置 // 这里使用简单的 block 级 scan (单 block，适合小规模) // 对于大规模数据，需要多级 scan // ========================================================================= __global__ void prefix_scan(int* __restrict__ data, int n) { __shared__ int sdata[2 * BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; // 加载数据到共享内存 (双缓冲，用于 Blelloch scan) sdata[tid] = (tid < n) ? data[tid] : 0; __syncthreads(); // Up-sweep (reduce) 阶段 int offset = 1; for (int d = n >> 1; d > 0; d >>= 1) { if (tid < d) { sdata[offset * (2 * tid + 2) - 1] += sdata[offset * (2 * tid + 1) - 1]; } offset *= 2; __syncthreads(); } // 清零最后一个元素 (exclusive scan) if (tid == 0) sdata[n - 1] = 0; __syncthreads(); // Down-sweep 阶段 for (int d = 1; d < n; d *= 2) { offset >>= 1; if (tid < d) { int t = sdata[offset * (2 * tid + 1) - 1]; sdata[offset * (2 * tid + 1) - 1] = sdata[offset * (2 * tid + 2) - 1]; sdata[offset * (2 * tid + 2) - 1] += t; } __syncthreads(); } // 写回 if (tid < n) data[tid] = sdata[tid]; }
Kernel 6
// ========================================================================= // 步骤 4b: 流压缩 (scatter) // 根据 scan 结果，将异常点的索引写入紧凑的输出数组 // output[scan[i]] = i (如果 flags[i] == 1) // ========================================================================= __global__ void compact_anomalies(const int* __restrict__ flags, const int* __restrict__ scan, int* __restrict__ output_indices, float* __restrict__ output_deviations, const float* __restrict__ deviations, int n) { int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; if (i < n && flags[i] == 1) { int out_idx = scan[i]; output_indices[out_idx] = i; output_deviations[out_idx] = deviations[i]; } }
Kernel 7
// ========================================================================= // 步骤 5: Bitonic Sort (并行排序网络) // 对异常点的偏离程度进行排序 (降序，最异常的在前) // Bitonic sort: O(log²N) 深度的并行排序网络 // 每个 block 处理一个序列 // ========================================================================= __global__ void bitonic_sort(float* __restrict__ values, int* __restrict__ indices, int n) { __shared__ float s_val[BLOCK_SIZE]; __shared__ int s_idx[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; // 加载数据 if (tid < n) { s_val[tid] = values[tid]; s_idx[tid] = indices[tid]; } else { s_val[tid] = -1e30f; // padding s_idx[tid] = -1; } __syncthreads(); // Bitonic sort 网络 for (int k = 2; k <= BLOCK_SIZE; k *= 2) { for (int j = k / 2; j > 0; j /= 2) { int ixj = tid ^ j; if (ixj > tid) { // 降序排序: 大的在前 // 比较方向由 k 的最高位决定 bool desc = ((tid & k) == 0); if ((desc && s_val[tid] < s_val[ixj]) || (!desc && s_val[tid] > s_val[ixj])) { // 交换 float tv = s_val[tid]; s_val[tid] = s_val[ixj]; s_val[ixj] = tv; int ti = s_idx[tid]; s_idx[tid] = s_idx[ixj]; s_idx[ixj] = ti; } } __syncthreads(); } } // 写回 if (tid < n) { values[tid] = s_val[tid]; indices[tid] = s_idx[tid]; } }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { const int N = BLOCK_SIZE * BLOCK_SIZE; // 65536 个数据点 const float threshold = 3.0f; // 3 标准差 printf("=== 并行异常检测 (APOD 全流程) ===\n"); printf("数据量: %d, 异常阈值: %.1f 标准差\n\n", N, threshold); // --- 生成测试数据 (正态分布 + 少量异常点) --- float* h_data = (float*)malloc(N * sizeof(float)); srand(42); for (int i = 0; i < N; i++) { // Box-Muller 变换生成正态分布 float u1 = (float)(rand() + 1) / (float)RAND_MAX; float u2 = (float)(rand() + 1) / (float)RAND_MAX; float z = sqrtf(-2.0f * logf(u1)) * cosf(2.0f * 3.14159265f * u2); h_data[i] = z; } // 注入异常点 int injected = 20; for (int i = 0; i < injected; i++) { h_data[rand() % N] = (rand() % 2 ? 1 : -1) * (5.0f + (float)(rand() % 50) / 10.0f); } // ==================== CPU 基准 (Assess) ==================== int* h_cpu_indices = (int*)malloc(N * sizeof(int)); float* h_cpu_deviations = (float*)malloc(N * sizeof(float)); int cpu_count = 0; cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); cudaEventRecord(start); cpu_anomaly_detection(h_data, N, threshold, h_cpu_indices, h_cpu_deviations, &cpu_count); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float cpu_ms = 0; cudaEventElapsedTime(&cpu_ms, start, stop); printf("--- CPU 基准 ---\n"); printf("CPU 耗时: %.3f ms, 检测到 %d 个异常点\n", cpu_ms, cpu_count); printf("前 5 个异常点: "); for (int i = 0; i < 5 && i < cpu_count; i++) { printf("[idx=%d, dev=%.2fσ] ", h_cpu_indices[i], h_cpu_deviations[i]); } printf("\n\n"); // ==================== GPU 实现 (Parallelize + Optimize) ==================== float *d_data, *d_dev_sq, *d_deviations; int *d_flags, *d_scan, *d_out_indices; float *d_out_deviations; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_data, N * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_dev_sq, N * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_deviations, N * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_flags, N * sizeof(int))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_scan, N * sizeof(int))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_out_indices, N * sizeof(int))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_out_deviations, N * sizeof(float))); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d_data, h_data, N * sizeof(float), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
编译 & 运行
# 编译 nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 week9_mastery/day59_anomaly_detection.cu -o week9_mastery/day59_anomaly_detection # 运行 ./week9_mastery/day59_anomaly_detection

毕业项目: 图像处理管线

week9_mastery/day60_final_capstone.cu
学习目标 & 核心概念
/* * day60_final_capstone.cu — 毕业项目: GPU 图像处理管线 * * 学习目标: * - 综合 60 天所学全部 CUDA 技术，构建完整图像处理管线 * - 使用 CUDA Graph 封装多 kernel 管线 * - 实现性能分析与结果可视化 * * 核心概念 (综合运用): * 1. 测试图像生成 (kernel + 随机数) * 2. 高斯模糊 (共享内存 + 常量内存) * 3. 灰度转换 (通道加权: 0.299R + 0.587G + 0.114B) * 4. Sobel 边缘检测 (共享内存 tiling + 卷积) * 5. 阈值二值化 (map kernel) * 6. 像素统计 (归约: 非零像素数 + 平均亮度) * + CUDA Graph 封装整个管线 * + 每 kernel 性能分析 * + ASCII art 输出 * * 对应官方指南: 全部 60 天综合 (BPG + PG 所有章节) * * 编译运行: * nvcc -O2 -std=c++14 -arch=sm_89 day60_final_capstone.cu -o day60 * ./day60 * * 这是 60 天 CUDA 学习的毕业项目，代码体现以下技术: * - 共享内存 tiling & halo region * - 常量内存 * - 归约 (reduction) * - map (element-wise) kernel * - 卷积 kernel * - CUDA Graph * - CUDA Event 计时 * - 错误处理 */
Kernel 函数代码
Kernel 1
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 1: 生成测试图像 (渐变 + 噪声) // 生成 RGB 图像，含渐变背景和随机噪声 // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_generate_image(float* __restrict__ img, int width, int height, int channels, unsigned long seed) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; if (x >= width || y >= height) return; // 简单的 LCG 随机数 unsigned long rng = seed + (y * width + x) * 1103515245UL; rng = (rng * 1103515245UL + 12345UL) & 0x7fffffffUL; float noise = (float)(rng % 100) / 1000.0f; // [0, 0.1) // 渐变 + 噪声 float r = (float)x / width + noise; float g = (float)y / height + noise; float b = (float)(x + y) / (width + height) + noise; int idx = (y * width + x) * channels; img[idx + 0] = r; img[idx + 1] = g; img[idx + 2] = b; }
Kernel 2
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 2: 高斯模糊 (共享内存 + 常量内存) // 可分离高斯模糊，先水平后垂直 // 这里为简化，直接用 2D 卷积 (单通道灰度图) // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_gaussian_blur(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int width, int height, int radius) { __shared__ float s_tile[TILE_SIZE + 2 * MAX_RADIUS] [TILE_SIZE + 2 * MAX_RADIUS]; int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; int sx = threadIdx.x + radius; int sy = threadIdx.y + radius; // 加载主区域 int gx = min(max(x, 0), width - 1); int gy = min(max(y, 0), height - 1); s_tile[sy][sx] = input[gy * width + gx]; // 加载 halo if (threadIdx.x < radius) { int hx = min(max(x - radius, 0), width - 1); s_tile[sy][threadIdx.x] = input[gy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.x >= TILE_SIZE - radius) { int hx = min(max(x + radius, 0), width - 1); s_tile[sy][sx + radius] = input[gy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.y < radius) { int hy = min(max(y - radius, 0), height - 1); s_tile[threadIdx.y][sx] = input[hy * width + gx]; if (threadIdx.x < radius) { s_tile[threadIdx.y][threadIdx.x] = input[hy * width + min(max(x - radius, 0), width - 1)]; } if (threadIdx.x >= TILE_SIZE - radius) { s_tile[threadIdx.y][sx + radius] = input[hy * width + min(max(x + radius, 0), width - 1)]; } } if (threadIdx.y >= TILE_SIZE - radius) { int hy = min(max(y + radius, 0), height - 1); s_tile[sy + radius][sx] = input[hy * width + gx]; if (threadIdx.x < radius) { s_tile[sy + radius][threadIdx.x] = input[hy * width + min(max(x - radius, 0), width - 1)]; } if (threadIdx.x >= TILE_SIZE - radius) { s_tile[sy + radius][sx + radius] = input[hy * width + min(max(x + radius, 0), width - 1)]; } } __syncthreads(); if (x < width && y < height) { float sum = 0.0f; for (int ky = -radius; ky <= radius; ky++) { for (int kx = -radius; kx <= radius; kx++) { float kv = c_gaussian[kx + radius] * c_gaussian[ky + radius]; sum += s_tile[sy + ky][sx + kx] * kv; } } output[y * width + x] = sum; } }
Kernel 3
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 3: 灰度转换 (通道加权) // gray = 0.299*R + 0.587*G + 0.114*B (ITU-R BT.601) // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_to_grayscale(const float* __restrict__ rgb, float* __restrict__ gray, int width, int height) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; if (x >= width || y >= height) return; int idx = (y * width + x) * CHANNELS; float r = rgb[idx + 0]; float g = rgb[idx + 1]; float b = rgb[idx + 2]; gray[y * width + x] = 0.299f * r + 0.587f * g + 0.114f * b; }
Kernel 4
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 4: Sobel 边缘检测 (共享内存 tiling) // Gx = Sobel_x * image, Gy = Sobel_y * image // magnitude = sqrt(Gx² + Gy²) // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_sobel(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int width, int height) { // 共享内存: TILE_SIZE + 2 (halo=1 for 3x3 Sobel) __shared__ float s_tile[TILE_SIZE + 2][TILE_SIZE + 2]; int x = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.y; int sx = threadIdx.x + 1; int sy = threadIdx.y + 1; int gx = min(max(x, 0), width - 1); int gy = min(max(y, 0), height - 1); s_tile[sy][sx] = input[gy * width + gx]; // halo (radius=1) if (threadIdx.x == 0) { int hx = min(max(x - 1, 0), width - 1); s_tile[sy][0] = input[gy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.x == TILE_SIZE - 1) { int hx = min(max(x + 1, 0), width - 1); s_tile[sy][TILE_SIZE + 1] = input[gy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.y == 0) { int hy = min(max(y - 1, 0), height - 1); s_tile[0][sx] = input[hy * width + gx]; } if (threadIdx.y == TILE_SIZE - 1) { int hy = min(max(y + 1, 0), height - 1); s_tile[TILE_SIZE + 1][sx] = input[hy * width + gx]; } // 角落 if (threadIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.y == 0) { int hx = min(max(x - 1, 0), width - 1); int hy = min(max(y - 1, 0), height - 1); s_tile[0][0] = input[hy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.x == TILE_SIZE - 1 && threadIdx.y == 0) { int hx = min(max(x + 1, 0), width - 1); int hy = min(max(y - 1, 0), height - 1); s_tile[0][TILE_SIZE + 1] = input[hy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.x == 0 && threadIdx.y == TILE_SIZE - 1) { int hx = min(max(x - 1, 0), width - 1); int hy = min(max(y + 1, 0), height - 1); s_tile[TILE_SIZE + 1][0] = input[hy * width + hx]; } if (threadIdx.x == TILE_SIZE - 1 && threadIdx.y == TILE_SIZE - 1) { int hx = min(max(x + 1, 0), width - 1); int hy = min(max(y + 1, 0), height - 1); s_tile[TILE_SIZE + 1][TILE_SIZE + 1] = input[hy * width + hx]; } __syncthreads(); if (x < width && y < height) { // Sobel 卷积 float gx_val = 0, gy_val = 0; for (int ky = -1; ky <= 1; ky++) { for (int kx = -1; kx <= 1; kx++) { float pixel = s_tile[sy + ky][sx + kx]; int kidx = (ky + 1) * 3 + (kx + 1); gx_val += pixel * c_sobel_x[kidx]; gy_val += pixel * c_sobel_y[kidx]; } } output[y * width + x] = sqrtf(gx_val * gx_val + gy_val * gy_val); } }
Kernel 5
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 5: 阈值二值化 (map kernel) // pixel > threshold → 1.0 (白), 否则 → 0.0 (黑) // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_threshold(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ output, int width, int height, float threshold) { int x = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; int y = blockIdx.y * blockDim.y + threadIdx.y; if (x >= width || y >= height) return; float val = input[y * width + x]; output[y * width + x] = (val > threshold) ? 1.0f : 0.0f; }
Kernel 6
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 6a: 像素统计 — 非零像素数 (归约) // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_count_nonzero(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ partial, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid; sdata[tid] = (i < n) ? ((input[i] > 0.0f) ? 1.0f : 0.0f) : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) { if (tid < s) sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + s]; __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) partial[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; }
Kernel 7
// ========================================================================= // Kernel 6b: 像素统计 — 平均亮度 (归约) // ========================================================================= __global__ void kernel_sum_brightness(const float* __restrict__ input, float* __restrict__ partial, int n) { __shared__ float sdata[BLOCK_SIZE]; int tid = threadIdx.x; int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + tid; sdata[tid] = (i < n) ? input[i] : 0.0f; __syncthreads(); for (int s = blockDim.x / 2; s > 0; s >>= 1) { if (tid < s) sdata[tid] += sdata[tid + s]; __syncthreads(); } if (tid == 0) partial[blockIdx.x] = sdata[0]; }
Main 函数 (Host 代码)
int main() { const int width = IMG_W, height = IMG_H; const int channels = CHANNELS; const size_t rgb_size = (size_t)width * height * channels * sizeof(float); const size_t gray_size = (size_t)width * height * sizeof(float); printf("=== 毕业项目: GPU 图像处理管线 ===\n"); printf("图像: %dx%d RGB → 灰度 → 边缘检测 → 二值化 → 统计\n\n", width, height); // --- 设置高斯核 --- int gauss_radius = 3; float gauss_sigma = 1.5f; setup_gaussian(gauss_radius, gauss_sigma); float threshold = 0.3f; // Sobel 阈值 // --- 分配设备内存 --- float *d_rgb, *d_blurred_rgb, *d_gray, *d_edges, *d_binary; float *d_partial; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_rgb, rgb_size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_blurred_rgb, rgb_size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_gray, gray_size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_edges, gray_size)); CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_binary, gray_size)); int n_pixels = width * height; int num_blocks = (n_pixels + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE; CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_partial, num_blocks * sizeof(float))); // --- 运行配置 --- dim3 block2d(TILE_SIZE, TILE_SIZE); dim3 grid2d((width + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE, (height + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE); // --- 逐 kernel 计时 --- cudaEvent_t start, stop; cudaEventCreate(&start); cudaEventCreate(&stop); printf("--- 各阶段执行时间 ---\n\n"); // 1. 生成图像 cudaEventRecord(start); kernel_generate_image<<<grid2d, block2d>>>(d_rgb, width, height, channels, 42); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float t_gen; cudaEventElapsedTime(&t_gen, start, stop); printf("1. 生成测试图像: %8.3f ms\n", t_gen); // 2. 高斯模糊 (对 RGB 每通道处理, 简化为先转灰度再模糊) // 为演示管线，先对 R 通道模糊到 d_blurred_rgb (简化) // 实际: 先灰度转换再模糊更合理，但为了展示 kernel 顺序多样性 // 这里先对灰度图模糊 // 先灰度转换 cudaEventRecord(start); kernel_to_grayscale<<<grid2d, block2d>>>(d_rgb, d_gray, width, height); cudaEventRecord(stop); cudaEventSynchronize(stop); float t_gray; cudaEventElapsedTime(&t_gray, start, stop);
